## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent identity, a clear contents page with slide ranges, section dividers and a logical route from business model to funding, sustainability, digitalisation and appendix. The readable slides (10, 14, 32) are well built: slide 10 pairs four charts with headline figures and one-line explanations, and slide 14 groups ratings by agency with the key grade in bold. The weaknesses are consequential: the long funding run (slides 19 to 27) repeats a dense chart-plus-table layout with tiny table text and rotated bond labels, slide 15 is crowded, and slide 35 has a broken footer and slide 50 a jumbled chart legend. Many pages are reference-grade rather than designed to be read live, and the five section dividers (4, 12, 31, 38, 41) add little.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the headline-figure-plus-chart structure of slide 10, the grouped rating layout of slide 14, the contents page with slide ranges, and the consistent blue and green identity with the repeating flag tiles (Total EUR, USD, GBP) on the benchmark pages.

**Most useful repair:** Enlarge or cut the small table text and rotated x-axis labels on slides 19 to 27 and 50, clean up the footer on slide 35 and the legend on slide 50, and thin the crowded slide 15.

**What would change the score:** I would add a takeaway headline to the repeated funding pages and a short summary slide for the live audience, moving the full tables to the appendix.

_Confidence medium. 58 of 58 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This deck is KfW's semi-annual investor presentation, dated November 2026 with data as of 31 October 2026. It opens with a disclaimer on US securities offers, followed by a table of contents. The business model section covers promotional volumes, the on-lending model, exposure by region and sector, and Frankfurt as a financial hub. Next, rating and funding cover the federal guarantee, financial and ESG ratings, and debt capital markets by currency and product. Later sections address sustainability, including SDG mapping, green bond allocation and impact, and digitalisation initiatives such as DLT-based bonds. The Germany Fund is also presented. The appendix contains the worldwide office network, liquidity portfolio, strategic holdings, key IFRS figures, Nordic and Asian funding, and sustainability governance. The deck closes with contact details and a photo credits slide. Several slides are dense tables of funding data and benchmark issues, serving as a reference for how an issuer lays out financial information. Slides 5 and 10 present key metrics as headline figures with short explanations, while slide 14 organises a long list of external ratings by agency and outlook.

## Questions about this deck

**How many slides does the KfW Bankengruppe investor presentation have?**

The deck has 58 pages, as stated in the deck's details. Slide 3, the table of contents, lists the final section, Contact & References, as running from slides 56 to 58.

**What does the KfW Bankengruppe deck say about its promotional business volume?**

Slide 10 states a total promotional business volume of €57.7bn, with about 67% domestic and 33% international business. Slide 7 gives the same H1/2026 promotional business volume of EUR 57.7bn.

**What credit ratings does the KfW Bankengruppe deck show?**

Slide 14 lists Moody's at Aaa, S&P at AAA, Fitch at AAA and Scope at AAA, each with a stable outlook. The same slide also shows the MSCI ESG rating of AAA.

**How is the KfW Bankengruppe investor presentation structured?**

Slide 3 sets out seven sections: Business Model (slides 4 to 11), Rating and Funding (slides 12 to 30), Commitment to Sustainability (slides 31 to 37), Digitalisation Initiatives (slides 38 to 40), The Germany Fund (slides 41 to 43), Appendix (slides 44 to 55), and Contact & References (slides 56 to 58).

Behind them is calm grey-blue water reflecting the quay, with a blurred port: a red-and-blue ship hull at far left, red and grey cranes, pale beige and white multi-storey buildings, and hazy hills under a pale peach sky at top. Composition is full-bleed with the bollards occupying the centre and right; the lower half is covered by the white panel. Lighting is soft and diffuse. **Visuals**

None apart from the seven navy section bars, their green number discs and the small white right-pointing arrows in front of each slide range; there are no photographs, charts or diagrams. ### Slide 4

**Text**

KfW
Business Model
Table of contents

**Layout**

Single full-bleed 16:9 slide with no visible grid. The title block (KfW over Business Model) is left-aligned at about 5.8% from the left edge and sits vertically around the middle, between 40% and 58% of the height. A small label, Table of contents, is right-aligned near the top right, ending at about 94% of the width and 7% to 10.5% from the top. There is no footer, page number or logo.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed decorative background: a dark-to-mid blue halftone texture. The left third is almost flat dark blue. From about x 25% to the right edge, a regular grid of small square dots in a slightly lighter blue fades in and grows denser toward the right edge, forming a checkerboard-like pattern. No photograph, chart, diagram or logo. Two very small 2 by 2 pixel image tiles are placed at x 5.7%, y 49.4% and x 5.7%, y 39.7%, behind the title text, and a third at x 76.5%, y 6.3% behind the header label; they read as flat colour and have no visible content of their own.

**Design**

Background is a dark blue field, #00446E on the left fading to #005A8C at the right, with a subtle square-dot halftone pattern in #00568A. Title KfW and the header Table of contents are light mint green #B5F5A0. Subtitle Business Model is the same mint green #B5F5A0 in a lighter weight. Typeface is a humanist sans-serif similar to Fira Sans; KfW is bold, Business Model is regular, Table of contents is semibold. Title cap height is about 6% of slide height (about 8.5% em); subtitle about 8% em; header about 4.2% em. Shapes: no outlines, no corner radii, no shadows and no gradient overlays beyond the background fade.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- illustration at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#00446E]: Full-slide background image: blue halftone square-dot pattern fading from flat dark blue on the left to a checkerboard-like pattern on the right (Covers the whole slide, full-bleed, behind everything; a white #FFFFFF fill also covers the slide beneath it and is hidden by the image; dots about #00568A on #005A8C and #00446E)
- shape at x 5.7, y 39.7, w 12.4, h 10.2 [#00446E]: Small 2 by 2 pixel image tile behind the title (Flat blue tile with no visible content; sits behind the KfW title; no border or shadow)

### Slide 5

**Text**

KfW at a glance
Our Legacy
KfW established in 1948
Shareholders: Federal Republic (80%)
& federal states (20%)
Our People & Network
>8,000 employees
Represented in over 80 offices worldwide
Our Business
Germany

& resilience

(2025)
€98bn

AAA

€40.6bn
Equity
27.7%
Capital ratio

Globalisation

Digitalisation & innovation

Commercial loans

Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
5

**Layout**

Four quadrant panels meet at a central green ring with a dark blue circle, separated by thin grey diagonal and vertical rules forming an X and a vertical divider at the slide centre. The title KfW at a glance sits top-left at about 3% from the top and left margins; section headings sit inside each quadrant, right-aligned in the top-left and bottom-left panels and left-aligned or centred elsewhere. The footer logo and footer line sit bottom-left, with the page number '5' bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four icon-and-label rows and two diagrams. (1) Our Business: a boxed organisation table with a dark blue header bar 'Germany' over a row with KfW (light blue-white cell, #E9F5FB) and KfW CAPITAL (pale blue cell); below, a row of four blue line icons with labels SMEs (cloud over factory), Private clients (house with people), Municipalities, LFI, companies (building with office blocks), Start-ups & Future Fund (rocket); then a dark green header bar 'International' over cells KfW IPEX-Bank (very pale green) and KfW DEG with KfW (light green), followed by green icons Export & project finance (euro coin) and Developing & emerging economies (globe). (2) Central diagram: concentric circles, pale green ring carrying curved text Strengthening competitiveness above and & resilience below, enclosing a dark blue disc with KfW wordmark in white and the text The digital transformation & promotional bank by 2030. (3) Our Key Financials: six blue figures with grey captions, no chart axes: €98bn New business, AAA Rating, €40.6bn Equity, 27.7% Capital ratio, €1.0bn Consolidated profit. (4) Our Focus: a horizontal row of five circular icons joined by a thin grey line: solar panel with sun on dark green (#398357) labelled Climate & environment; globe on bright green (#57D96D) labelled Globalisation; shuffle arrows on light green (#B7F9AA) labelled Trend-independent promotion topics; people group on pale green (#D4FCCA) labelled Social change; gear with circuit on very pale green (#ECFDED) labelled Digitalisation & innovation. (5) Our Products: a row of five circular icons joined by a thin grey line: house with euro sign on navy (#00446E) labelled Promotional loans; hand with plant on dark blue (#005A8C) labelled Grants; coin stacks on bright blue (#007ABC) labelled Commercial loans; handshake on light blue (#97CFED) labelled Strategic holdings; hand with pen-like tool on pale blue (#D1EBF7) labelled Other. (6) KfW wordmark logo bottom-left, dark blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with four pale blue-white quadrant panels of #F2F8FC. Flat, clean corporate look with very pale blue-white panels and a central dark blue disc. Title in bold dark blue (#005A8C) sans-serif (DIN-like humanist grotesque), about 6.5% of slide height; section headings in bold dark blue #005A8C about 4% of slide height; body text in regular dark grey (#3C3C3C) sans-serif about 2.8% of slide height, with key numbers in bold. Financial figures in bold blue (#0078B4) about 3.5% of slide height. Accent colours: dark blue #005A8C, bright blue #007ABC, mid green #57D96D, dark green #266446 and #398357, pale green #D4FCCA. Thin grey (#A0AAB4) hairline rules, 1 px, no shadows, no gradients, no rounded corners apart from the circles.

### Slide 6

**Text**

Transformation agenda ‘KfWplus’ / Strengthening competitiveness & resilience / Climate & / environment / Digitalisation & / innovation / Managing impact & / mobilising private / capital / Customer / Top-performing KfW / 1. Making a difference, / together. / 3. Digitalisation & AI / 2. Employee / potential / 4. Business & / operating model, / and governance / The digital / transformation / & promotional / bank / KfW / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 6 / 6

**Layout**

Full-bleed navy slide with the title top-left at about 3.5% from the top and 2.7% from the left. A circular wheel of four quadrants sits centre-right, with a white Customer cross at its centre, a white circle with text at the top-right, the KfWplus wordmark at left-middle, and a footer row at the bottom with the KfW logo, footer line and page number at bottom-right. Margins are about 3% on the left and right.

**Visuals**

Illustrated quadrant diagram. A circle is divided into four quarter segments by a navy cross. Top-left quadrant is light green (#CCF5C8) with a flat illustration of green trees, a grey trunk, white and grey clouds and a yellow sun over a dark green hill, labelled Climate & environment. Top-right quadrant is light blue (#9ACFEB) with a white robotic arm on a blue cloud (#0A6DB5) beside a small navy dashboard with green and white bar chart, labelled Digitalisation & innovation. Bottom-left quadrant is light grey (#D0D3D9) with a stack of grey and white euro banknotes (one marked €), a circular arrow loop and a stack of grey coins with a euro coin, labelled Managing impact & mobilising private capital. Bottom-right is a light blue box (#D4ECF7) titled Top-performing KfW with four numbered items: 1 Making a difference together with two cartoon people and a hex-shaped cluster, 3 Digitalisation & AI with a laptop showing a document and sparkles, 2 Employee potential with a raised arm and a dark blue disc, 4 Business & operating model, and governance with a flag and rising bar chart in blues. A thick blue (#0A6DB5) curved arc runs around the outside of the wheel and carries the heading Strengthening competitiveness & resilience. A blue arrow points up and right toward the white circle. The arcs and arrow are flat with rounded ends and no shadows.

**Design**

Background: Solid deep navy #003F6B covering the whole slide. Flat corporate infographic style on a deep navy background. Title is white bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger/Myriad-like), about 5.5% of slide height. Body labels are bold dark navy or dark text at about 2.6% of slide height. Quadrant labels use dark green #1E5E3A for climate, dark blue #0A4C7C for digitalisation, and dark grey #3C4048 for managing impact. Accent blues are #0A6DB5 and #0A4C7C. Shapes are flat with no gradients, no shadows and sharp inner corners, except the box corner which is slightly rounded (about 1% radius). Footer text is white regular sans-serif at about 1.9% of slide height.

Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
7

**Layout**

Six-cell grid in three columns and two rows, with two rotated row labels (Domestic on top, International below) to the left of the grid; the title sits top-left at about 2.7% margin, with the subtitle directly beneath it, and the content grid spans roughly x 10% to 94% and y 15% to 80%. Each cell pairs a coloured header bar, a description, a photo or statistic and a donut chart; the right column holds two green KPI rows with grey notes. A footer row carries the KfW logo at bottom left, the footer text beside it and the page number 7 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Five photographs and five donut charts, plus two KPI tiles and two logos. Photos: (1) SME cell, bottom-left: solar panels on a red-tiled roof under a pale blue sky, cropped rectangle, daylight, blue and red dominant. (2) Customized Finance cell: overhead view of an open-plan office with people at round tables and green plants, cropped rectangle, bright neutral light, white, beige and green dominant. (3) KfW Capital cell: three business people at a white flipchart, one woman in the centre, two men in dark suits, indoor soft light, grey, white and dark navy dominant. (4) Developing countries cell: a young girl in a pink-and-white striped dress pumping water from a concrete well in a courtyard with a pink wall and blue curtain, daylight, pink and grey dominant. (5) Export cell: a red-hulled container ship moored beside a glass-fronted port building under a blue sky, daylight, red, teal and blue dominant. Donut charts, all with a white ring, grey outline and light green (#B7F9AA) arc equal to the printed share, with the percentage printed in the centre in bold Arial #41484C: SME Bank 56% (green arc about 56% of ring, starting at 12 o'clock clockwise); Customized Finance 10%; KfW Capital 1%; Developing Countries 7%; Export & Project Finance 26%. KPI tiles: 'KfW Group’s Environment investment ratio (H1/2026)' with value 58%, and 'KfW Group’s Domestic SME ratio (H1/2026)' with value 45%, each a light green box with bold dark blue label and a green value box. Logos: KfW CAPITAL wordmark in bold dark blue KfW with smaller CAPITAL beside it, and KfW IPEX-Bank wordmark in bold dark blue KfW with smaller IPEX-Bank beside it; KfW logo in footer. No other visuals.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate investor design on white. Header bars are dark navy #00446E for the domestic row and mid green #3C8A5C for the international row, with white bold sans-serif headings about 2.5% of slide height. Title is bold sans-serif (Frutiger-like humanist sans) in #00446E at about 5.2% of slide height; subtitle is regular weight in #00446E at about 3.8%. Body text is regular sans-serif in #41484C at about 2.2% of slide height. Domestic description panels are pale blue #E9F5FB; international panels are pale mint #ECFDED. KPI boxes are light green #B7F9AA with dark blue #00446E bold labels and values; notes are on #F2F2F2 with grey #41484C Arial text about 1.6% of slide height. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients; donut outlines are thin grey. Row labels are rotated 90 degrees in bold, Domestic #00446E and International #3C8A5C.

### Slide 8

**Text**

Proven and successful business model
KfW involves commercial banks in its domestic activities (on-lending model)
Liquidity management
KfW
BANK
Lender
Financing partner
Final borrower
The claim of the house bank against the final
borrower is assigned/ceded to KfW as collateral
Funding
KfW
Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
8

**Layout**

Left-aligned header: bold title at the top-left with a subtitle directly beneath it, both starting at about 2.7% from the left edge. Below, a wide horizontal diagram fills the middle band, with a circular globe-and-cycle illustration on the left and a three-stage flow (KfW, BANK, Final borrower) on the right, with a green return arrow beneath the stage bars. A footer strip at the bottom holds the KfW logo at the left, footer text beside it and the page number at the right margin; there are no boxes or grid lines around the content.

**Visuals**

Diagram (one illustration, roughly 82% of slide width, placed at x 6.4%, y 21.6%). Left part: a dark blue (#00578A) globe with light blue-grey (#9FC3D9) continents, wrapped by two light grey (#D6DCE0) curved block arrows forming a circular cycle. Above the globe are stacked coins: a green (#3D8054) pound coin, a dark green euro coin and a light green (#8FE38A) dollar coin, labelled 'Liquidity management' in green text. Below the globe are similar stacked coins (pound, euro, light green dollar) labelled 'Funding'. To the left of the globe sit a dark green server/tower block with white horizontal slots, a second smaller dark green server, and a light green house with four white windows. Centre: a tall rounded dark blue (#00578A) rectangle with three white horizontal bars and the white bold text 'KFW' at its base. A light grey (#D6DCE0) block arrow points right to a white-backed square containing a dark blue (#00578A) house-shaped 'BANK' icon with white bold capitals and a small light blue chimney tab at its top right; a second light grey arrow points right to the borrower illustration. Right part: a light blue (#55B0E6) house with four white windows, a dark green wind turbine with light green curved motion arcs, a light green sun over a dark green solar panel, a dark green pine tree and a light green round bush at the lower left. Bottom: a dark green (#3D8054) thick U-shaped arrow runs from beneath the 'Final borrower' bar, along the bottom, and up into an upward-pointing arrowhead under the 'Lender' bar. The arrow is solid, with no shadows or gradients. Labels 'Lender', 'Financing partner' and 'Final borrower' sit in three dark blue (#00578A) rectangular bars with white bold text, and the caption in dark grey bold text sits inside the U of the arrow. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White (#FFFFFF) background, flat with no gradient or texture. Title in bold dark blue #00578F, humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger Bold, at about 5.2% of slide height (cap height about 3.6%). Subtitle in regular weight of the same blue #00578F, humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger Roman, about 3.1% of slide height. Diagram accents: dark blue #00578A, mid green #3D8054, light green #8FE38A, light blue #55B0E6, light grey #D6DCE0, light blue-grey #9FC3D9. Stage labels white bold on dark blue bars; caption dark grey #4A4A4A bold, about 2.2% of slide height. Footer text grey #555555 at about 1.7% of slide height, page number same grey. Shapes have square corners on bars and rounded corners on the KfW tower. No shadows.

**Layout**

Two side-by-side donut charts in the lower-middle area below a two-line title block, each with a legend to its right and a KPI box pair beneath it. Title is left-aligned at about 2.7% from the left edge and top 3.2%, subtitle directly beneath it at top 9.9%. Left-aligned footer row at y 95.7% with KFW logo at left, footer text and page number '9' at the far right margin (about 96.6% x). Content is organised in two columns, the left block spanning x 9.6% to 46%, the right block x 54.9% to 90%.

**Visuals**

Two doughnut charts with white separator lines between segments, no axes, no legend-embedded title. Left chart titled 'By region', subtitle '2025', centre of ring at about x 20.7%, y 47.5%. Segments clockwise from 12 o'clock with labelled values: Germany 33%, Euro-area countries (excl. Germany) 23%, EU countries (excl. euro-area countries and Germany) 5%, Europe outside EU 9%, Africa 3%, Asia (incl. Australia and New Zealand) 10%, Latin America 6%, North America 11%; values sum to 100%. Right chart titled 'By sector', subtitle '2025', centre at about x 65.5%, y 47.5%. Segments clockwise from 12 o'clock: Banking sector 38%, Industrial sector 15%, Retail sector 6%, Non-basic consumer goods 9%, Utility companies 9%, Financial sector 8%, No sector focus 4%, Other 11%; values sum to 100%. Legends are square swatches with labels, one per row. No highlighted points beyond the Germany and banking segments referenced by the KPI boxes. Two KPI callouts: 'Exposure to Germany:' with value 33%, and 'Exposure to banking sector:' with value 38%, each a navy box with white bold label next to a pale green box with dark green bold value.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger-like), navy #005A8C, approx 5.1% of slide height. Subtitle in regular weight of the same family, navy #005A8C, approx 3.6% of slide height. Chart headings 'By region' and 'By sector' in bold dark slate #3F4850, about 2.5% of slide height; years '2025' in regular weight slate #3F4850, about 2.0%. Legend and data labels in regular weight, about 1.9% of slide height, dark grey #4A4F55 for legend text and white or dark tone on segments. Palette: navy #005A8C, dark navy #00375B, azure #0076C4, light blue #97CFED, bright green #33C414, pale mint #B7F9AA, dark green #398357, slate grey #6D767D. Footer text grey #5A5F66 about 1.8% of slide height. Flat shapes, no shadows, no gradients, square corners on KPI boxes.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Four small charts with one headline figure each and a one-line explanation beneath, a clean way to present a multi-year trend in a single view.
- Review: The readable slides (10, 14, 32) are well built: slide 10 pairs four charts with headline figures and one-line explanations, and slide 14 groups ratings by agency with the key grade in bold.
- Worth keeping: Keep the headline-figure-plus-chart structure of slide 10, the grouped rating layout of slide 14, the contents page with slide ranges, and the consistent blue and green identity with the repeating flag tiles (Total EUR, USD, GBP) on the benchmark pages.

10

**Layout**

Four equal boxed panels in a 2 by 2 grid below a single left-aligned title at top left. Each panel holds one bar chart on the left (about 55 percent of panel width) and a headline KPI, a green divider line and a caption on the right. Title sits at top left with left margin about 2.7 percent. Footer runs along the bottom with the KFW logo at bottom left, footer text next to it and the page number 10 at bottom right. Panels leave about 3 percent margins on left and right.

**Visuals**

Four bar charts, all with grey (#BFBFBF) bars and a single highlighted bar for H1/2026. Chart 1 (top left): stacked bar chart titled by the KPI Total promotional business volume, axis unit in bn €, no visible numeric axis, bars for 2019 to H1/2026 with printed totals 77,3 / 135,3 / 107,0 / 166,9 / 111,3 / 112,8 / 98,0 / 57,7. Grey base segments; 2020 has a light green #B7F9AA Covid segment on top, 2021 a small green segment, 2022 a large blue #0070C0 energy segment plus small green, 2023 and 2024 blue energy segments, 2025 a thin blue segment. Legend below: blue square Mandated transactions energy; light green square Mandated transactions Covid. Chart 2 (top right): simple column chart titled Total assets, unit in bn €, values 506 / 546 / 551 / 555 / 561 / 545 / 541 / 550, H1/2026 bar in blue #007ABC, others grey. Chart 3 (bottom left): column chart in bn €, values 1,4 / 0,5 / 2,2 / 1,4 / 1,6 / 1,4 / 1,0 / 0,9 for 2019 to H1/2026, H1/2026 bar blue #007ABC, others grey; a horizontal dashed blue line (#007ABC, about 1 pt) crosses the chart at about the level of the 0,9 bar top with no value printed; legend dashed blue line Strategic target (before IFRS effects from hedging). Chart 4 (bottom right): column chart in %, values 21,3 / 24,1 / 23,9 / 25,0 / 27,9 / 30,2 / 27,7 for 2019 to 2025 in grey, H1/2026 split: blue #0070C0 top segment labelled 28,1 above, and a lower grey segment with dashed outline labelled 14,4 inside; a dashed blue horizontal reference line in legend Regulatory requirement. KFW logo in dark blue bold sans wordmark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background, pale light-blue panels #E9F5FB at 70 percent opacity with no borders and square corners. Title in bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger-like) dark blue #005A8C, large. KPI headlines in bold dark blue #005A8C; KPI captions in regular dark blue #005A8C. Body text in regular dark grey #3C3C3C. Chart numbers and axis labels in mid grey #595959, regular weight. Accent: light green #8DE27A divider lines with a small downward V notch at centre; bright blue #0070C0 and #007ABC for highlights; light green #B7F9AA for Covid segments. Title about 6 percent of slide height, KPI headlines about 5 percent, captions and body about 2.2 percent, chart labels about 1.6 percent. No shadows, no gradients.

**Layout**

Full-bleed 16:9 slide split into quadrants: the title spans the full width across the top at about 3.6% from the top edge, with a 2.4% left margin. The top-left and bottom-right quadrants carry pale blue fills, while the top-right and bottom-left quadrants stay white. A circular photo is centred on the slide at the quadrant intersection, with the left column and right column each left-aligned to a shared text edge (left column at about 3.4% x, right column at about 52.9% x). The footer runs along the bottom-left with the KfW logo at left, and the page number sits at the bottom-right corner at about 97.6% x.

**Visuals**

One circular photograph of the Frankfurt skyline, centred at about x 37.7 y 32.7 w 22.9 h 41.5 percent. Subject: a dense cluster of high-rise office towers (glass and concrete, several tall slender towers in the centre and right) rising above a lower foreground of older mid-rise residential buildings with pitched roofs and a few trees in spring leaf. Composition: wide view from an elevated position, skyline occupying the upper-middle of the circle, the rooftops in the lower third; the image is cropped to a circle with a thin dark grey outline of about 0.75 pt. Lighting: soft, diffuse daylight under a pale hazy sky. Dominant colours: pale blue-grey sky (#C9D6E2), grey-beige concrete, muted green tree tones, and steel-blue glass. No overlay or tint. Also: a KfW wordmark logo at the footer, and a set of KPI icons in the lower right: a house with people figures (dark blue #00517F roof outline with light blue #3E9BD6 figures) above 781,337; a person seated at a desk with a laptop (dark blue head and body, light blue #3E9BD6 laptop) above 89,366; a globe with a pencil (dark blue globe with light blue accent) above the >800 years text; a graduation cap with a book (dark blue #00517F, light blue tassel) above 249,096; a magnifying glass over a line chart with nodes (dark blue handle, light blue arrows) above 44; a dark blue rectangular tile (#00517F) with a light blue euro coin badge, a building outline and a small house icon above 280. No other charts, tables or diagrams.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with two pale blue fields #F2F8FC: the top-left quadrant and the bottom-right quadrant. Titles and section headings are dark blue #00517F; body text is dark grey #2D3033; accent highlights within bullets and the KPI numbers use medium blue #0A7DC2; logos are the same medium blue #0A7DC2. Typefaces: the title and headings are a bold humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger Bold; body text is a regular-weight sans-serif similar to Arial. Title size is about 5.3 percent of slide height (cap height about 3.6 percent); section headings about 3.0 percent; body bullets about 2.3 percent; KPI numbers about 2.8 percent bold; KPI labels about 1.9 percent regular. Lines are simple, with no rules or borders on the slide. Shapes are rectangles with square corners. No shadows or gradients. Bullets are small round dark grey dots with hanging indent.

### Slide 12

**Text**

Table of contents
KfW Rating and Funding
Capital and Money Markets

**Layout**

Single-screen section divider with a left-aligned text block: the title and subtitle sit at the left margin (about 5.7% of slide width) around the vertical middle, stacked on two lines. The 'Table of contents' label sits in the upper right, starting at about 76.5% of width and 6.3% of height. There is no visible footer, page number or logo.

**Visuals**

A halftone pattern of small square dots fills the right side of the slide. The dots are darker navy squares on a slightly lighter blue ground, with the density highest at the right edge and fading to nothing toward the left, where the ground is plain. No photograph, illustration, chart, logo or icon.

**Design**

Background is a deep blue (#00456E) with a right-side tonal dot pattern (#005A8C dots on #00507F). Title and subtitle text is light mint green (#B5F5A3). The 'Table of contents' label is the same mint green. Typeface is a humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger: the title is bold, the subtitle is regular weight, and the contents label is semibold. Title size is about 8.5% of slide height (cap height around 0.06 of slide height), subtitle about 7.8%, and the contents label about 4.2%. Shapes are plain with no corner radii, shadows or gradients.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#00456E]: Full-slide background fill (solid deep blue, covers the slide) (Solid fill with no border, behind all other elements.)
- illustration at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#005A8C]: Halftone square-dot pattern on the right side of the slide, fading to plain blue toward the left (Square dots in a regular grid, darker navy and lighter blue tones, density increasing toward the right edge; sits over the background fill and under the text; opacity about 100% on dots with the fade produced by dot size and density; no border or shadow.)

### Slide 13

**Text**

Guarantee from the / Federal Republic of Germany
Guarantee established in 1998
Defined by law
Direct, explicit and unconditional
§1a of the Law concerning KfW
The Federal Republic guarantees all obligations of KfW in respect of loans extended to and debt securities issued by KfW, fixed forward transactions or options entered into by KfW and other credits extended to KfW as well as credits extended to third parties inasmuch as they are expressly guaranteed by KfW.
KfW
Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
13

**Layout**

Two-column split. The left half (0 to 50% of width) is a full-bleed photograph running the full slide height. The right half is a white text panel with a 3% right margin. The title is centred at the top of the panel, followed by three icon-and-text rows, a thin horizontal rule, a blue subheading and a left-aligned body paragraph starting at x 53%. The KfW logo and footer line sit bottom-left over the photograph; the page number sits bottom-right in the white panel.

**Visuals**

One photograph and three small line icons. Photograph: the Reichstag building in Berlin seen from the front at a low angle, centred slightly left. The glass dome is at upper left, the pediment with sculptural relief is centred, the inscription 'DEM DEUTSCHEN VOLKE' is on the frieze above the columns, and a row of stone columns rises above people on the steps. A German flag hangs on a pole at right and a partial flag appears at the far left edge. Daylight with a clear pale blue sky; dominant colours are sky blue #8FC3E0, sandstone beige #B9AE98, glass grey #7D8A90 and black flag stripes. The photo is full-bleed and not cropped in a box, with no overlay or tint. Icons: (1) a padlock in a filled blue circle #005A8C, white glyph; (2) a graduation cap in a filled grey circle #6D6E71, white glyph; (3) a clipboard with a check mark in a filled green circle #3A8A3F, white glyph. A thin grey horizontal rule #8C8C8C, about 1 px, runs across the right panel below the bullets.

**Design**

Background is plain white #FFFFFF on the right panel with a full-height photograph on the left. Title and subheading are bold blue #005A8C in a humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger Bold, with the title in two centred lines at about 5.0% of slide height and the subheading at about 4.2%. Body and bullet text are regular dark grey #4A4A4A in a Frutiger-like sans-serif, bullets at about 3.7% and body at about 2.2% of slide height. Icons are filled circles about 5% of slide height in diameter with no shadow. Footer and logo are white over the photo. Page number is grey #4A4A4A, about 1.9%.

### Slide 14

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Financial and ESG ratings are grouped in a single grid with agency logos, outlook, and short-term rating, making a dense comparison easy to scan.
- Review: The readable slides (10, 14, 32) are well built: slide 10 pairs four charts with headline figures and one-line explanations, and slide 14 groups ratings by agency with the key grade in bold.
- Worth keeping: Keep the headline-figure-plus-chart structure of slide 10, the grouped rating layout of slide 14, the contents page with slide ranges, and the consistent blue and green identity with the repeating flag tiles (Total EUR, USD, GBP) on the benchmark pages.

**Text**

Financial and ESG ratings¹ from leading rating agencies / KfW assigned triple-A ratings from financial rating agencies and is among top-performers in ESG / Financial ratings from leading rating agencies / Sustainability ratings confirm KfW’s holistic approach / Largest public development bank with strong capitalization, solid asset quality and conservative risk profile / Aaa Outlook stable / Short-term: P-1 / Strong and explicit linkages between KfW and the Federal Republic of Germany / AAA Outlook stable / Short-term: S-1+ / Key national development bank with a crucial role for executing key German economic policies / AAA Outlook stable / Short-term: A-1+ / Unsolicited rating / KfW has a broad and long-lasting policy role as Germany‘s largest development bank / AAA Outlook stable / Short-term: F-1+ / The Prime status was awarded based on to KfW’s performance above the industry-specific Prime threshold. / KFW / A+ / B- / D- / Prime / 24/08/2026 / AAA in the MSCI ESG Ratings assessment. / KFW / AAA / CCC / 07/09/2026 / 2 / 1 A rating is not a recommendation to buy, sell or hold securities. Ratings are subject to revision or withdrawal at any time by the assigning rating organization. Each rating should be evaluated independently of any other rating. / 2 The use by KfW of any MSCI ESG Research LLC or its affiliates (“MSCI”) data, and the use of MSCI logos, trademarks, service marks or index names herein, do not constitute a sponsorship, endorsement, recommendation, or promotion of KfW by MSCI. MSCI services and data are the property of MSCI or its information providers, and are provided ‘as-is’ and without warranty. MSCI names and logos are trademarks or service marks of MSCI. / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 14

**Layout**

Full-width 16:9 slide with the title block at top-left, a 2-part section header row (dark blue left panel about 46% wide, light green right panel about 46% wide) at about 18% to 24% of slide height, and a grid of rating cards below: left 2 by 2 cards for financial agencies (Moody's, Scope, S&P, Fitch) in the left column group, and right 1 by 2 cards for ISS ESG and MSCI under the green header. Titles and body are left-aligned with a left margin of about 2.7% and right edge about 95%; the footnotes sit at the bottom, the KfW logo bottom-left with footer text beside it, and the page number 14 bottom-right at about 96% x and 96% y.

**Visuals**

Logos: Moody's wordmark in dark navy serif capitals (x 6.0, y 28.7, w 10.3, h 3.9); Scope logo, a grey square box with the word SCOPE in dark serif capitals (x 28.7, y 27.3, w 4.9, h 6.3); Standard & Poor's wordmark in black serif capitals with a thin red rule (x 5.8, y 58.5, w 6.7, h 5.3); Fitch Ratings logo with 'Fitch' in crimson #C8102E and 'Ratings' in grey (x 32.5, y 60.0, w 9.3, h 3.5); ISS ESG logo with 'ISS' and 'ESG' in navy and green plus a blue arrow and the tagline 'ethix · climate · oekom' (x 50.2, y 27.1, w 11.1, h 6.8); MSCI logo in grey-blue letters with a globe icon and a small superscript 2 (x 73.2, y 26.9, w 10.3, h 5.9). Chart in ISS card: vertical dark green bar #266446 (x about 67.9 to 68.8, y about 32.9 to 51.3) with scale labels A+ at top, B- at middle and D- at bottom (ordinal rating scale, no numeric values); KFW marker logo with a small blue arrow pointing to the bar at about the B- level (x 63.7 to 67.3, y 39.5 to 41.5). Chart in MSCI card: vertical dark green bar #266446 (x about 90.9 to 91.7, y about 32.9 to 51.3) with labels AAA at top and CCC at bottom; a KFW logo with blue arrow pointing to the top AAA level (x 87.1 to 90.5, y 32.6 to 34.2). No legend, no highlighted points beyond the KFW markers. Footer KfW logo: dark blue #005A8C bold sans wordmark (x 2.7, y 94.5, w 4.1, h 3.0).

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background with no gradient or texture. Titles and subtitle in KfW blue #005A8C; the title is bold sans-serif (Frutiger-like), subtitle is regular sans-serif. Header bars: left solid #005A8C with white regular sans text; right solid light green #B7F9AA with dark blue #005A8C text. Rating cards: square corners, no shadows, no borders; financial cards filled #E9F5FB, Fitch card filled #EEF0F2 light grey, sustainability cards filled #ECFDED. Body text is italic light sans-serif in dark grey #3C3C3C; the ISS body text is italic in green #266446. Rating grades are large bold sans in dark grey #3C3C3C (about 4.5% of slide height for the grade word, body around 2% and labels around 1.7%). 'Outlook stable' and 'Short-term' lines are medium-weight sans about 2.2% of height. Prime and ISS grade text bold green #266446. Footnotes are small regular sans about 1.0% in dark grey #3C3C3C. Footer text is regular sans in #3C3C3C about 1.9%.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: the long funding run (slides 19 to 27) repeats a dense chart-plus-table layout with tiny table text and rotated bond labels, slide 15 is crowded, and slide 35 has a broken footer and slide 50 a jumbled chart legend.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the small table text and rotated x-axis labels on slides 19 to 27 and 50, clean up the footer on slide 35 and the legend on slide 50, and thin the crowded slide 15.

**Text**

Capital markets / SSA market / #2 in the SSA segment / Expected funding volumes 2026 in / € billion / Rentenbank 11 / NRW.Bank 15-17 / KfW 80-85 / EU 180 / EIB 60 / IBRD 44-53 / ADB 36 / ESM/EFSF 25 / IDB 17 / IFC 18-26 / IADB 17 / BNG 15-17 / EBRD 16 / Our USPs / Credit risk of the Federal Republic with yield pick-up vs Bunds – even in non-euro currencies (surrogate to Bund) / Liquidity of our bonds / Currency & product diversity / Relevance / Green bonds 17.5% / €433 billion / One of the largest and most active bond issuers worldwide / Funding volume 2025: €71bn / Outstanding KfW bonds & SSD at end of 2025 / Credit ratings – Aaa / AAA / AAA / KfW / Guarantee / Institutional liability / Risk weight 0% / Sustainability ratings – Prime / AAA / Green bonds / Active for more than 10 years / Among the top 5 issuers worldwide / In 2026, KfW exceeded the €100 billion mark in total KfW green bonds issued / Digitalisation / KfW makes an important contribution to shaping the digital financial market as an active bond issuer and investor / Strengthening Europe's digital sovereignty, e.g., European Regulated Layer One (RL1) initiative, ECB-AG Pontes / Currency diversity / In 2025, KfW issued bonds in 10 currencies / 58% / 24% / 10% / 8% / EUR / USD / GBP / AUD HKD NOK / CHF SEK CNY / DKK / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 15

**Layout**

Six-panel radial layout: a central green ring with a navy circle at the centre, and five pale-blue quadrant panels around it (top-left, top-right, left, right, bottom) separated by thin grey diagonal lines and a vertical divider at the slide centre. The title Capital markets sits top-left at about 3.5% from the top and 2.5% from the left edge; the footer line sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right at about 95.5% from the top.

**Visuals**

Chart: horizontal bar chart titled Expected funding volumes 2026 in € billion, placed top-centre-left under the SSA market heading. Categories top to bottom: Rentenbank 11; NRW.Bank 15-17; KfW 80-85 (highlighted in dark navy #005A8C, bar is the only dark bar); EU 180; EIB 60; IBRD 44-53; ADB 36; ESM/EFSF 25; IDB 17; IFC 18-26; IADB 17; BNG 15-17; EBRD 16. Bars are light blue #97CFED with bold dark labels at bar ends; category labels right-aligned in grey #41484C; vertical baseline axis line in dark grey; no visible axis tick values, value labels printed at bar ends. Bar length scale is proportional to value, with EU 180 the longest bar. Legend: none. Photos: six square-cropped photographs in a 2-row grid, bottom-left of the slide, boxed with square corners and no border: (1) offshore wind turbines against a dusk sky shaded blue to orange; (2) a white and beige multi-storey apartment building with a red-tiled roof and green trees under a blue sky; (3) a yellow and black tram on a street lined with dark trees, seen from the side; (4) a ring-tailed lemur sitting on a branch with a soft green background; (5) a worker in a blue shirt installing dark blue solar panels on a roof. Icon grid: a 2-column, 3-row cluster of small square UN Sustainable Development Goals icons (about six visible) with colours yellow, orange, red, dark green, blue and green, to the right of the tram photo. Logos: KfW wordmark in white inside the central navy circle; KfW wordmark in navy at bottom-left. Diagram: a circular text ring with a light green band around a navy disc, the top arc reads Credit ratings – Aaa then AAA and AAA, the bottom arc reads Sustainability ratings – Prime then AAA. Donut chart titled Relevance: outer ring with navy #005A8C segment (82.5%) and light green #D4FCCA segment (Green bonds 17.5%) at top right of ring; centre label €433 billion; caption Outstanding KfW bonds & SSD at end of 2025. Currency bar chart titled In 2025, KfW issued bonds in 10 currencies: a single 100% horizontal stacked bar with segments EUR 58% (navy #005A8C, label white), USD 24% (light blue #97CFED, label navy), GBP 10% (pale green #B7F5A4, label navy), and 8% (bright green #5BD46B, label navy) which stands for AUD HKD NOK CHF SEK CNY DKK; category names EUR, USD, GBP below segments and the grouped list beside the last segment. Lines: thin grey diagonal lines from the central ring to the four panel corners and a vertical grey divider at the slide centre.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with pale blue #F2F8FC quadrant panels covering the slide. Clean corporate look: white base with very pale blue #F2F8FC quadrant panels; titles and headings in bold navy #005A8C; body text in dark grey #41484C; accents light green #D4FCCA, pale blue #97CFED, bright green #5BD46B and dark green text #2E7D4F for the ring labels and Green bonds label. Typefaces: a humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger or Myriad, title bold, headings bold, body regular. Title about 5.2% of slide height, section headings about 3.4%, body text about 2.3%, footer about 1.6%. Shapes: the central circle and donut are perfectly round with no shadows; panel edges are straight diagonals with hairline grey strokes about 0.5 px; photos have square corners and no shadow.

### Slide 16

**Text**

Strong presence in DCM / DCM funding / in billion euros / 82,6 / 89,4 / 90,2 / 78,0 / 71,0 / 83,8 / 5,4 / 5,4 / 5,4 / 5,1 / 4,7 / 5,2 / 16,2 / 10,6 / 12,9 / 12,2 / 14,0 / 21,4 / 2021 / 2022 / 2023 / 2024 / 2025 / 2026 / (YTD) / Green Bonds / Average duration in years / KFW / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 16 / EUR 80-85bn / Funding target for 2026 / Total funding volume expected at EUR 80-85bn / Up to EUR 15bn funding via Green Bonds with continued focus on EUR market / Refinancing strategy for 2026 continues unchanged: / EUR and USD remain our core refinancing currencies / Currency diversification upon investor demand, e.g. new growing markets like HKD

**Layout**

Two-column split: a full-height navy left panel about 49% of the slide width holds the title, subtitle and chart, and a white right panel holds the headline, a divider line and the bullet list. The left title sits top-left at about 2.7% margin; the right headline is centred in the right panel. Footer (KFW logo and footer line) sits bottom-left of the navy panel and the page number sits bottom-right of the white panel.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical stacked column chart with a secondary line overlay, titled 'DCM funding in billion euros' (white text on navy). Bars are light blue #97CFED (total DCM funding, values printed above each bar, in billion euros) with light green #B7F9AA segments at the base (Green Bonds, values printed inside each segment). Categories on the horizontal axis: 2021, 2022, 2023, 2024, 2025, 2026 (YTD). Values: 2021 total 82,6 with Green Bonds 16,2; 2022 total 89,4 with Green Bonds 10,6; 2023 total 90,2 with Green Bonds 12,9; 2024 total 78,0 with Green Bonds 12,2; 2025 total 71,0 with Green Bonds 14,0; 2026 (YTD) total 83,8 with Green Bonds 21,4. No y-axis or gridlines are printed; the value scale is about 0 to 95 billion euros implied by bar heights (estimate). A thin white baseline runs under the bars. Line series 'Average duration in years' in dark blue #005A8C, drawn across the green segments with white rounded-oval callouts containing the printed values: 5,4 (2021), 5,4 (2022), 5,4 (2023), 5,1 (2024), 4,7 (2025), 5,2 (2026). Legend below the chart: green swatch labelled Green Bonds and a dark blue line sample labelled Average duration in years. No highlighted points beyond the callouts. Right panel contains a thin dark blue horizontal divider with a small downward V-notch at its centre; no photograph, logo other than the KFW wordmark in the footer.

**Design**

Background: Split solid: navy #00446E on the left 49% of the slide and white #FFFFFF on the right 51%. Two-tone: navy #00446E left panel and white #FFFFFF right panel. Title in white #FFFFFF, bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger-like), about 5.6% of slide height. Subtitle and chart labels in white #FFFFFF regular weight, about 2.7% and 1.9%. Right headline in dark blue #005A8C bold sans, 'EUR' and 'bn' smaller weight, '80-85' about 11.5% of slide height, 'Funding target for 2026' bold about 5.6%. Bullets in dark blue #005A8C regular sans about 3.3% of slide height with bullet glyphs in the same colour. Bars light blue #97CFED and light green #B7F9AA; line dark blue #005A8C. Flat, no shadows, no gradients, square corners on bars; callouts are white ovals with thin dark blue #005A8C outline.

17

**Layout**

Single slide with a title across the top left and a three-column text block on the left (about 75 percent of width) with a column chart panel on the right. The three columns (KfW Benchmark Programmes, Additional Public Bonds, Tailor-made Placements) sit side by side with coloured header bars at the same top edge; the chart panel sits at the far right. A large green-gradient block fills the lower area beneath the columns, with a centred bold quote near its bottom. Left margin about 2.7 percent; the footer row holds the KfW logo at left, footer text, and page number 17 at far right.

**Visuals**

Column chart, titled EUR bn (label at top right of the panel), with no axis lines or gridlines and no axis tick values. One series, shown as three bars with the categories grouped under the label 2026 (YTD). Bar 1 KfW Benchmark Programmes, dark navy #002D46, value 58,7 printed above the bar (about 58.7 EUR bn). Bar 2 Additional public bonds, blue #005A8C, value 18,5 printed above (18.5 EUR bn). Bar 3 Tailor-made placements, bright blue #0076C4, value 6,6 printed above (6.6 EUR bn). Bar heights are proportional to the values (bar 1 about 42.7 percent of slide height, bar 2 about 13.4, bar 3 about 4.8). Legend below the bars in dark grey text with small square markers in the series colours, listed in order: KfW Benchmark Programmes, Additional public bonds, Tailor-made placements. No highlighted points. Behind the columns, a green-to-grey gradient rectangle, and three faint grey watermark currency-symbol strings at about 20 percent opacity: '€ $' at left, '€ $ £ HKD' and 'A$ SEK NOK' at right. Header bars are flat rectangles with square corners and no shadow.

**Design**

Background is white at the slide level with a light blue to pale green gradient panel behind the body. Titles in dark cerulean blue #00598C, bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger-like), title size about 6.3 percent of slide height. Body text in dark slate grey #41484C, regular Arial-like sans-serif, about 2.6 percent of slide height. Column header text in white bold sans-serif, about 3.2 percent of slide height. Accent colours: #00446E, #005A8C, #0076C4 for headers and bars; #002D46 for the darkest bar. The quote is bold sans-serif in deep green #245C3F, about 4.2 percent of slide height. Footer text grey #5A6268, about 1.8 percent of slide height. Shapes have square corners, no borders, no shadows. Gradient runs from pale blue #EAF5FB at top through grey #B9C4CA in the middle to light green #B4F7A0 at the bottom.

Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
18

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: the title sits top-left at about 2.7% margin, with the subtitle beneath it; a centred bold chart heading sits above two side-by-side bar charts (left EUR/USD chart narrower, right GBP-to-Others chart wider). A full-width pale-blue callout band sits in the lower third, and a footer row carries the KfW logo at bottom-left, the footer text next to it, and the page number 18 at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Two grouped vertical bar charts with a shared legend style. Chart: vertical stacked bar chart, one stacked column per year from 2026 to 2036, 11 columns evenly spaced. Title: none printed inside the chart. Y-axis: 'EUR bn', range 0 to 90, gridline labels 10,00 steps (labels read '-' at zero, 10,00, 20,00 ... 90,00), no gridlines visible. X-axis: years 2026 to 2036 as category labels. Series (stacked bottom to top: EUR dark navy, USD green, Others light blue), values are not printed on the slide so all are estimates marked about: 2026 EUR about 0, USD about 3.2, Others about 3.8, total about 7.1. 2027 EUR about 60.7, USD about 15.1, Others about 7.7, total about 83.5. 2028 EUR about 52.8, USD about 16.8, Others about 12.5, total about 82.1. 2029 EUR about 45.1, USD about 12.9, Others about 7.2, total about 65.2. 2030 EUR about 29.0, USD about 10.4, Others about 4.7, total about 44.1. 2031 EUR about 29.7, USD about 10.8, Others about 4.7, total about 45.2. 2032 EUR about 28.4, USD about 0.4, Others about 3.7, total about 32.5. 2033 EUR about 18.2, USD about 6.5, Others about 0.3, total about 25.0. 2034 EUR about 13.8, USD about 1.7, Others about 0.4, total about 15.9. 2035 EUR about 9.7, USD about 0.1, Others about 0.2, total about 10.0. 2036 EUR about 14.8, USD about 3.2, Others about 1.0, total about 19.0. **Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field. The title 'EUR Benchmark Programme' sits top-left above the left column, and a green callout box sits top-right. The left column holds a blue 'Highlights' header bar, a light-blue bullet panel and a bordered benchmark table. The right column holds the 'Outstandings' stacked column chart, with 'bn EUR' above its y-axis, the legend below it and the chart's year groups beneath. The KfW logo sits bottom-left, the footer line follows it, and the page number 20 sits bottom-right; the left column is left-aligned and the margins are about 2.6% of the width.

**Visuals**

Chart (stacked column, 'Outstandings', y-axis 'bn EUR' from 0 to 8 in steps of 1, no x-axis title). The x-axis carries one column per bond with rotated labels, grouped under the years 2027 (8 bars), 2028 (7), 2029 (5), 2030 (3), 2031 (4), 2032 (1), 2033 (2), 2034 (1), 2035 (1), 2036 (3) and 2037 (1). Values are estimates ('about') because no values are printed. Bars from left to right, stacked bottom to top: 2027/02 about 5 New Issue; 2027/05 about 5 New Issue plus 2 Tap; 2027/04 about 5 New Issue plus 1 Tap; 2027/06 about 5 New Issue plus 2 Tap; 2027/08 about 6 New Issue plus 1 Tap; 2027/09 about 5 New Issue; 2027/10 about 4 New Issue, 2 Tap, 1 Tap 2026; 2027/12 about 3 New Issue plus 3 Tap; 2028/01 about 5 New Issue; 2028/03 about 6 New Issue plus 1 Tap; 2028/04 about 6 New Issue plus 1 Tap; 2028/06 about 4 New Issue plus 2 Tap; 2028/07 about 3 New Issue, 1 Tap, 2 Tap 2026; 2028/10 about 4 New Issue plus 3 Tap; 2028/11 about 5 New Issue plus 2 Tap; 2029/01 about 5 New Issue plus 2 Tap; 2029/04 about 5 New Issue plus 2 Tap; 2029/06 new about 5 New Issue plus 1 New Issue 2026; 2029/09 new green about 5 New Issue 2026 Green; 2029/12 about 4 New Issue plus 1 Tap; 2030/06 about 5 New Issue plus 2 Tap 2026; 2030/09 about 3 New Issue plus 2 Tap; 2030/11 about 3 New Issue; 2031/01 about 5 New Issue plus 1 Tap; 2031/02 about 4 New Issue plus 1 Tap; 2031/06 new green about 5 New Issue 2026 Green; 2031/10 about 3 New Issue plus 3 Tap 2026; 2032/01 about 5 New Issue plus 2 Tap; 2033/04 new green about 5 New Issue 2026 Green; 2033/06 about 3 New Issue, 1 Tap, 1 Tap 2026; 2034/01 about 3 New Issue, 3 Tap, 1 Tap 2026; 2035/01 about 3 New Issue plus 3 Tap; 2036/01 new about 5 New Issue 2026; 2036/05 about 3 New Issue; 2036/06 new about 3 New Issue 2026; 2037/03 about 2 New Issue plus 2 Tap. Table: columns '€ BM', '€ bn', 'Green', '2026', 'Tenor', '%', 'Lead Managers'; rows I/2026 5.0 N Jan 7 3y 2.375 BofA, LBBW, HSBC, Barclays; II/2026 5.0 N Jan 7 10y 3.0 BofA, LBBW, HSBC, Barclays; III/2026 5.0 Y Feb 4 7y 2.75 BNP, DB, GS, Danske; IV/2026 5.0 Y Apr 16 5y 2.875 CoBa, NatWest, Santander, UBS; V/2026 3.0 N May 27 10y 3.375 BNP, HSBC, DZ, GS; VI/2026 (tap VI/2024) 3.0 N June 30 7y 2.5 CA, Deka, JPM, MS; VII/2026 5.0 Y Aug 25 3y 3.0 Barclays, DB, NatWest, SocGen; footer row Re-openings 12.0, 2026 Total 43.0. **Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and the KfW logo are in dark blue #005A8C, the callout's euro sign and text are in dark navy #004369, and body text is dark grey #41484C. The accent is KfW blue #005A8C for the header bar, table rules and chart's New Issue series. Typefaces: the title and headings are a bold humanist sans (Frutiger-like), body text a regular sans-serif (Frutiger-like), and the callout and euro sign an Arial-like sans. The title is about 6.5% of slide height; body text about 1.9% of slide height. Line and shape styles: the header bar and callout are square-cornered flat fills with no shadow; the bullet panel is a flat light-blue box; the table has thin dark blue rules under the header and above the footer row; chart gridlines are thin light grey horizontal lines. No gradients.

### Slide 21

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: the long funding run (slides 19 to 27) repeats a dense chart-plus-table layout with tiny table text and rotated bond labels, slide 15 is crowded, and slide 35 has a broken footer and slide 50 a jumbled chart legend.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the small table text and rotated x-axis labels on slides 19 to 27 and 50, clean up the footer on slide 35 and the legend on slide 50, and thin the crowded slide 15.

**Layout**

Two equal columns separated by a thin vertical grey rule at about x 50%. Title is top-left at x 2.7% with a subtitle beneath it; a large euro symbol sits top-right. Each column has a coloured header bar, then bullets, then a chart; the left column stacks two line charts, the right column has a bar-line chart, a paragraph and an italic note. Footnotes sit at the bottom left above the KfW logo and footer line; the page number is bottom right at about x 96%.

**Visuals**

Four charts. (1) Line chart 'Geographic distribution (in %)1': y-axis 0 to 80 in steps of 20 (percent), x-axis 2019 to 2026, light grey gridlines. Series, values about: Europe ex Germany (dark blue #005A8C) about 64, 67, 68, 66, 58, 67, 62, 54; Germany (pale green #B7F9AA) about 22, 20, 14, 14, 18, 12, 10, 6; Asia (green #57D96D) about 12, 8, 13, 12, 15, 13, 15, 10; Other (light blue #9ED3F0) about 3, 6, 5, 10, 9, 5, 12, 19. Legend to the right in the same order, labels in grey. (2) Line chart 'Investor distribution (in %)1': y-axis 0 to 50 in steps of 10 (percent), x-axis 2019 to 2026. Series, values about: Central Banks (dark blue) about 28, 35, 36, 27, 29, 36, 32, 37; Banks (pale green) about 38, 36, 24, 23, 42, 33, 32, 28; Asset Manager (green) about 25, 23, 31, 39, 21, 23, 27, 23; Ins. & Pension (light blue) about 3, 1, 4, 9, 5, 5, 4, 4; Other (navy) about 6, 6, 5, 3, 3, 4, 4, 8. Legend to the right, top to bottom in the same order. (3) Bar and line chart headed 'EUR' (no axes shown, values printed): pale green bars Total Outstandings (aggregate principal) 2017 98.0, 2018 124.0, 2019 138.5, 2020 172.5, 2021 195.0, 2022 216.0, 2023 227.0, 2024 229.0, 2025 254.0; dark green bars New Issuance (aggregate principal) 2017 32.5, 2018 37.5, 2019 41.5, 2020 32.5, 2021 33.8, 2022 41.3, 2023 34.0, 2024 35.6, 2025 27; dark blue line Total Turnover 2017 144.0, 2018 147.0, 2019 157.8, 2020 116.4, 2021 123.9, 2022 148.1, 2023 147.6, 2024 165.9, 2025 205.6. Bars are grouped by year on a shared baseline; no highlighted points. Legend at right with three swatches (pale green bar, green bar, dark blue line). (4) Euro symbol logo in dark blue #004369, bold. Also a KfW wordmark logo bottom-left in dark blue #005A8C.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Title is a bold humanist sans (Frutiger-like) in dark blue #005A8C, about 5.5% of slide height for cap height; subtitle is regular weight in #005A8C at about 3.6% of slide height. Left header bar is solid #005A8C with white bold sans text about 2.5% of height. Right header bar is pale green #B7F9AA with dark green bold text #1E5631. Body text is regular sans in dark grey #41484C at about 2.3% of slide height. Chart text grey #6E7378. Accent colours are pale green #B7F9AA, green #57D96D and blue #005A8C. Lines are 2-3 px strokes with no markers; header bars have square corners and no shadow; the divider is a 1 px grey rule #7F7F7F. No gradients or shadows.

### Slide 24

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: the long funding run (slides 19 to 27) repeats a dense chart-plus-table layout with tiny table text and rotated bond labels, slide 15 is crowded, and slide 35 has a broken footer and slide 50 a jumbled chart legend.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the small table text and rotated x-axis labels on slides 19 to 27 and 50, clean up the footer on slide 35 and the legend on slide 50, and thin the crowded slide 15.

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field: a narrow left column (about 2.6% to 39% of slide width) holds two stacked blue-header panels and a table, and a wide right column (about 41% to 98%) holds the Outstandings column chart. The title 'KfW in GBP' sits top-left at about 3% from the top with the subtitle beneath it; a green callout box sits top-right. The KfW logo and footer line sit bottom-left, and the page number '24' sits bottom-right, all on a shared bottom margin of about 4% of slide height. Text is left-aligned throughout; the table and chart values are centred.

**Visuals**

Chart: vertical stacked column chart titled 'Outstandings' with y-axis label 'GBP bn'. Y-axis runs 0 to 4 with gridlines and labels at 0, 0.5, 1, 1.5, 2, 2.5, 3, 3.5, 4; no value labels are printed on the bars, so all values below are about. Eighteen columns grouped under year labels: 2026 (1 column), 2027 (3), 2028 (4), 2029 (2), 2030 (2), 2031 (2), 2032 (2), 2036 (1), 2037 (1). Category labels are rotated about 45 degrees: KfW GBP 2026/12; KfW GBP 2027/07; KfW GBP 2027/10 green; KfW GBP 2027/12; KfW GBP 2028/01; KfW GBP 2028/09 new; KfW GBP 2028/10; KfW GBP 2028/12; KfW GBP 2029/01; KfW GBP 2029/10; KfW GBP 2030/02 green; KfW GBP 2030/10; KfW GBP 2031/02 green; KfW GBP 2031/07 new; KfW GBP 2032/06; KfW GBP 2032/06 new; KfW GBP 2036/06; KfW GBP 2037/03. Stacked segments, bottom to top, with about values: 2026/12 New Issue about 1.0, Tap about 2.45 (total about 3.45). 2027/07 New Issue about 0.5, Tap about 1.85 (total about 2.35). 2027/10 green New Issue Green about 0.75, Tap 2026 Green about 0.25 (total about 1.0). 2027/12 New Issue about 0.75, Tap about 0.6, Tap 2026 about 0.15 (total about 1.5). 2028/01 New Issue about 1.75, Tap about 0.45 (total about 2.2). 2028/09 new New Issue 2026 about 0.75. 2028/10 New Issue about 0.5, Tap about 1.8 (total about 2.3). 2028/12 New Issue about 2.95. 2029/01 New Issue about 1.25, Tap about 0.65, Tap 2026 about 0.8 (total about 2.7). 2029/10 New Issue about 0.65, Tap 2026 about 0.55 (total about 1.2). 2030/02 green New Issue Green about 0.5, Tap 2026 Green about 0.4 (total about 0.9). 2030/10 New Issue about 0.6, Tap 2026 about 0.95 (total about 1.58). 2031/02 green New Issue Green about 0.5. 2031/07 new New Issue 2026 about 2.0, Tap 2026 about 0.3 (total about 2.3). 2032/06 New Issue about 0.8, Tap about 0.7 (total about 1.5). 2032/06 new New Issue 2026 about 0.6, Tap 2026 about 0.6 (total about 1.2). 2036/06 New Issue about 0.1, Tap about 0.6 (total about 0.7). 2037/03 New Issue about 0.15, Tap about 0.15 (total about 0.3). No highlighted points. Legend beneath the axis in one row: New Issue (dark blue square), New Issue 2026 (purple), New Issue Green (green), Tap (light blue), Tap 2026 (lilac), Tap 2026 Green (checkered green and grey square). Table: 6 columns (2026, GBP bn, Green, Tenor, Coupon (%), Lead Managers) and rows as follows: Jan 05 | 2.0 | N | 5y | 4.0 | BMO, Citi, HSBC, NatWest; Mar 06 | 0.75 | N | 2y | SONIA + 22 BP | NatWest, RBC, TD; June 12 | 0.60 | N | 6y | 4.5 | BoA, MS, RBC; Re-openings | 4.03; 2026 Total | 7.38. Table has no fill, thin dark blue horizontal rules above the header, below the header and at the bottom. Logo: KfW wordmark in dark blue at bottom-left. Callout: green speech-bubble shape top-right containing 'Total 29.4bn' and a sterling sign. Photographs and illustrations: none. The file also lists about 50 tiny 2 by 2 pixel image fragments placed across the table, chart labels and footer; they are rendering fragments and are not separate visuals.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in dark blue #005A8C (bold); subtitle in regular-weight dark blue #005A8C. Section header bars filled #005A8C with white bold text. Body text dark grey #41484C, regular weight. Left panel light blue tint #E9F5FB with no border. Callout box fill #B7F9AA with text and sterling sign in dark navy #004369. Chart colours: New Issue #005A8C, New Issue 2026 #A455C5, New Issue Green #57D96D, Tap #97CFED, Tap 2026 #C99AE1, Tap 2026 Green checkered #57D96D over grey #7E8A90. Typeface: a humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger, in regular and bold weights. Title text about 5% of slide height; subtitle about 3.4%; section headers about 3%; body bullets about 2.1%; table text about 1.9%; footer about 1.9%. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients; table rules are thin dark blue lines.

### Slide 25

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: the long funding run (slides 19 to 27) repeats a dense chart-plus-table layout with tiny table text and rotated bond labels, slide 15 is crowded, and slide 35 has a broken footer and slide 50 a jumbled chart legend.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the small table text and rotated x-axis labels on slides 19 to 27 and 50, clean up the footer on slide 35 and the legend on slide 50, and thin the crowded slide 15.

**Text**

KfW in AUD / KfW is one of the largest SSA Kangaroo issuers / Total / 16.2bn / A$ / Characteristics / KfW is among the leading SSA Kangaroo issuers in terms of outstanding volume and new issuance volume / KfW is a strong supporter of the Australian green bond market / KfW maintains its strategic approach to the Kangaroo market and is a regular and agile issuer / KfW's Kangaroo bonds are RBA repo eligible / Highlights 2026 / AUD remains in TOP 5 of funding currencies / KfW raised already AUD 4.45bn (ytd) in the Kangaroo market / KfW added a new liquid 5y green bond to its curve / KfW actively supported the liquidity of its curve via 10 reopenings already / 2026 / AUD bn / Green / Tenor / Coupon (%) / Lead Managers / Jan 08 / 1.5 / Y / 5y / 4.6 / Nomura, RBC, TD / Aug 14 / 0.5 / N / 4y / 4.8 / JPM, RBC, UBS / Re-openings / 2.45 / 2026 Total / 4.45 / Outstandings / AUD bn / New Issue / New Issue 2026 / New Issue 2026 Green / New Issue Green / Tap / Tap Green / Tap 2026 / Tap 2026 Green / 2027 / 2028 / 2029 / 2030 / 2031 / 2032 / KfW AUD 2027/02 / KfW AUD 2027/07 / KfW AUD 2028/03 / KfW AUD 2028/05 green / KfW AUD 2029/02 / KfW AUD 2029/07 green / KfW AUD 2030/01 green / KfW AUD 2030/08 new / KfW AUD 2031/02 / KfW AUD 2031/06 new green / KfW AUD 2032/02 / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 25

**Layout**

Two-column slide with a narrow left column (about 2.6% to 39% of the slide width) holding stacked text panels and a table, and a wide right column (about 43% to 97%) holding a single bar chart with its legend below. The title 'KfW in AUD' sits top left at about 3.8% from the top, with the subtitle directly beneath it, both left-aligned on a margin of about 2.7%. A green callout box sits top right. The KfW logo and footer line sit along the bottom left and the page number sits bottom right at about 96%.

**Visuals**

Bar chart titled 'Outstandings' with the unit label 'AUD bn' at top left of the axis. Vertical axis runs from 0 to 3,5 in steps of 0,5, with horizontal light grey gridlines. Estimated stacked values, read from the bars as 'about' values because no values are printed: KfW AUD 2027/02 New Issue about 0,3 and Tap about 0,7 total. KfW AUD 2027/07 New Issue about 0,8 and Tap about 2,3 total. KfW AUD 2028/03 New Issue about 0,15, Tap about 1,3 total, Tap 2026 about 1,4 total. KfW AUD 2028/05 green New Issue Green about 0,65 and Tap Green about 1,3 total. KfW AUD 2029/02 New Issue about 0,55, Tap about 1,55 total, Tap 2026 about 2,4 total. KfW AUD 2029/07 green New Issue Green about 1,5, Tap Green about 1,75 total, Tap 2026 Green about 2,0 total. KfW AUD 2030/01 green New Issue Green about 1,25, Tap Green about 2,35 total, Tap 2026 Green about 3,0 total. KfW AUD 2030/08 new New Issue 2026 about 0,5 and Tap 2026 about 0,8 total. KfW AUD 2031/02 New Issue about 0,3. KfW AUD 2031/06 new green New Issue 2026 Green about 1,5 and Tap 2026 Green about 1,8 total. KfW AUD 2032/02 New Issue about 0,2. Category labels are rotated about 45 degrees. Year groups 2027, 2028, 2029, 2030, 2031, 2032 are printed beneath the categories with divider lines between groups. Legend in two rows below the chart: New Issue, New Issue 2026, New Issue 2026 Green, New Issue Green; Tap, Tap Green, Tap 2026, Tap 2026 Green. Series colours: New Issue dark blue, New Issue 2026 purple, New Issue 2026 Green light green, New Issue Green bright green, Tap pale blue, Tap Green green and lavender checkerboard, Tap 2026 lavender, Tap 2026 Green magenta and green checkerboard. The checkered segments are tiled 2 by 2 pixel pattern fills. No point is highlighted. Table with columns 2026, AUD bn, Green, Tenor, Coupon (%), Lead Managers and rows: Jan 08 | 1.5 | Y | 5y | 4.6 | Nomura, RBC, TD; Aug 14 | 0.5 | N | 4y | 4.8 | JPM, RBC, UBS; Re-openings | 2.45 | blank | blank | blank | blank; 2026 Total | 4.45 | blank | blank | blank | blank. The KfW logo in dark blue is at bottom left. The green callout has a small downward pointing tail.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style on white. Titles and headings in KfW blue #005A8C. Subtitle in the same blue, regular weight. Body text dark slate grey #41484C. Accent: blue header bars #005A8C with white bold text, a green callout #B7F9AA with navy text #004369 and #00355F, and a light blue panel #E9F5FB. Typefaces: title is a bold humanist sans-serif close to Frutiger or the KfW corporate face; body is a regular sans-serif close to Frutiger; the chart and table use Arial-like sans-serif. Title size about 5.2% of slide height, subtitle about 3.9%, section headings about 2.5%, body about 2.1%, footer about 2.0%, chart labels about 1.7%. Square corners on bars and panels, thin dark blue rules around the table, no shadows or gradients.

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: the long funding run (slides 19 to 27) repeats a dense chart-plus-table layout with tiny table text and rotated bond labels, slide 15 is crowded, and slide 35 has a broken footer and slide 50 a jumbled chart legend.

**Text**

KfW in HKD
KfW’s commitment to Wonton
Highlights
•
In 2026, KfW issued the first Wonton with local settlement
via the Central Moneymarkets Unit (CMU) – an HKD 6 bn,
3y issue in benchmark size.
•
Largest bond ever issued by a European public sector
issuer in the developing Wonton market
KfW’s second public HKD benchmark following an
inaugural transaction in 2025
Final orderbook garnered demand in excess of HKD
9.2bn (including HKD 4.5bn JLM interest) from 21
accounts
•
Focus on targeting and attracting new Asian investors who
prefer a stable and secure currency combined with the new
distribution channel of local settlement.

**Layout**

The title KfW in HKD sits top-left at about 3% from the top edge with the subtitle directly beneath it; a green callout sits top-right. The table is at the bottom of the left panel, and the footer runs along the bottom with the KfW logo at left and the page number 26 at bottom-right, about 2.5% from the bottom edge and 3% from the right edge. Text is left-aligned in the left column; chart labels are centred under the bars.

**Visuals**

1) Bar chart titled Outstandings, with a y-axis labelled HKD bn ranging from 0 to 7 in steps of 1, with light grey horizontal gridlines at each unit. Three bars, no printed values: bar 1 KFW HKD 2028/09 (2028) dark blue, about 3 HKD bn; bar 2 KFW HKD 2029/01 new (2029) lilac, about 6 HKD bn, the tallest bar; bar 3 KFW HKD 2031/09 new (2031) lilac, about 4 HKD bn. The file also records a lilac fill #C99AE1 at x 67.1, y 33.5, w 23.6, h 48.1 that spans the two lilac bars. 2) Table with columns 2026, HKD bn, Green, Tenor, Coupon (%), Lead Managers and rows: Jan 20 | 6.0 | N | 3y | 2.882 | BoA, HSBC, StanChart; Sep 18 | 3.0 | N | 3y | 2,796 | BoA, HSBC; 2026 Total | 6.0 | blank | blank | blank | blank. Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style on white. Title KfW in HKD is bold sans-serif in dark blue #005A8C, about 5.6% of slide height. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients.

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: the long funding run (slides 19 to 27) repeats a dense chart-plus-table layout with tiny table text and rotated bond labels, slide 15 is crowded, and slide 35 has a broken footer and slide 50 a jumbled chart legend.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the small table text and rotated x-axis labels on slides 19 to 27 and 50, clean up the footer on slide 35 and the legend on slide 50, and thin the crowded slide 15.

Two-column layout on a white field with the title block across the top left: the title and subtitle are left-aligned at about 2.7% from the left edge, and a light green total badge sits at the top right, about 2.7% from the top and 2.8% from the right edge. The footer runs along the bottom with the KfW logo at the left margin and the footer text beside it; the page number 28 is right-aligned near the bottom-right margin. Panels align to a common top edge at about 13% and end near 90% of slide height.

**Visuals**

Chart: stacked vertical bar chart, eight bars for years 2019 to 2026, titled 'Net proceeds 2026: EUR 18.8 billion' (chart title printed above the plot, dark grey bold). ### Slide 29

**Text**

Money market activities
Large ECP / USCP programmes for short term liquidity
ECP programme
Multi-currency commercial paper (ECP) programme with a programme size of EUR 90bn (since August 2022)
Flexible in all maturities, starting with 1 week
Sizes start from EUR 2.5m and are not capped
In 2025, KfW has issued 794 ECPs with a total nominal volume of EUR 89bn
USCP programme
USD CP programme with a size of USD 30bn (since March 2022)
Flexible in all maturities including same-day funding; max. USD 0.75bn maturities per day
In 2025, KfW has issued 493 USCPs with a total nominal volume of USD 57bn
Top CP-Ratings
P-1 (Moody's)
S-1+ (Scope)
A-1+ (S&P)
Tailor-made solutions
KfW aims to meet investors' needs by showing flexibility in sizes, maturities and currencies (wide currency range possible in ECP programme)
Variety of currencies
2%
14%
19%
65%
Oktober 2026
USD
EUR
GBP
Others
In 2026 YTD, KfW has issued CPs in 7 currencies under its ECP programme:
USD, EUR, GBP, AUD, CHF, NZD and HKD
Overall, CPs were issued in 17 currencies since the launch of the programme
KfW
Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
29

**Layout**

Three-column grid under a full-width title block: the title 'Money market activities' sits top-left at about 4% of slide height with a subtitle beneath it. Column one (ECP programme) spans x 2.7 to 27; column two (USCP programme) spans x 29.8 to 54; column three (Tailor-made solutions and Variety of currencies) spans x 56.7 to 97.3, with thin vertical grey divider lines between columns. Headers are left-aligned at a common top of about 22.5%. The KfW logo sits bottom-left with the footer line beside it, and the page number '29' sits bottom-right at about 97%.

**Visuals**

Coloured header bars carrying small white or grey icons: ECP programme header has a house-shaped icon with a euro symbol; USCP programme header has a stack of coins icon in grey; Tailor-made solutions header has a handshake icon in white; Variety of currencies header has a globe icon in white. Top CP-Ratings panel is a dark blue box with a white icon of a rating/chart symbol: a line graph with markers above a magnifier-like pin. Donut chart titled 'Oktober 2026' in the centre, with segments: USD 65% (dark blue #005A8C, large segment on the right and bottom), EUR 19% (light green #B7F9AA, left), GBP 14% (green #57D96D, upper left), Others 2% (light blue, top, with a leader line label). Legend below the chart in order USD, EUR, GBP, Others with matching square markers. No axes or numeric values beyond the printed percentages. Highlighted points: none.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in dark blue #005A8C, body text in dark grey #41484C, accent colours dark blue #005A8C, light green #B7F9AA, mid green #398357 and grey #6D767D. Typeface is a humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger or Myriad, title in bold at about 7% of slide height, subtitle regular at about 4% of slide height, body text regular at about 2.5% of slide height with bold runs for key figures. Header bars are flat rectangles with no corner radius, no shadows and no gradients. The Top CP-Ratings box has a downward-pointing triangular tab at its bottom edge.
