### Slide 30

**Text**

Capital market awards: Celebrating KfW’s achievements / Global Finance / “Safest bank award 2026” / Global Finance names the 100 Safest Banks in the World / 1 KfW Germany / Winner of the “Safest Bank Award” in 2026—for the 18th consecutive year / Eligible for the award are the world’s largest 500 banks by asset size / Calculated based on long-term foreign currency ratings issued by Moody’s, S&P and Fitch Ratings / Environmental Finance / “Green bond of the year–agency 2024” / Sustainable Debt Awards 2024 Winner / Green bond of the year – agency / Bond Awards 2023 Winner / Green bond of the year – agency / Bond Awards 2022 Winner / Green bond of the year – agency / CMD-Capital Markets Daily / “Best SSA Issuer” / “Best GBP Issuer” / “Most Innovative Issuer” / “Best ESG IR-Team” / 2026 Best SSA Issuer KFW cmdportal / 2026 Best ESG Investor Relations Team KFW cmdportal / 2026 Most Innovative Issuer KFW cmdportal / 2026 Best GBP Issuer KFW cmdportal / GlobalCapital / “Bond & MTN Market Awards” / Bond Awards 2026 GlobalCapital WINNER KfW Most Impressive Agency Funding Official / Overall Most Impressive SSA Funding Team / Most Impressive SSA Issuer at Using New Bond Market Technology / MTN Awards 2026 GlobalCapital Your market. Your vote. Your winners. WINNER Agency Issuer of the Year MTN 10: Deal of the Year KfW - Rmb750m 2.15% April 2030 green – Bank of America / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / October 2026 / 30

**Layout**

Full-bleed navy slide split into four equal white columns of equal height, left to right, sitting in a band from about 18% to 90% of slide height, with a 3% side margin. The title sits top-left above the columns, left-aligned, at about 4% from the top. Each column has a centred heading at the top, a centred quote in the middle, and its evidence (logos, screenshot, or text) below. A KfW logo sits bottom-left with the footer line beside it, and the page number 30 sits bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Four white award panels, each with award imagery. Column 1 (Global Finance): a dark navy screenshot with light text reading Global Finance names the 100 Safest Banks in the World, a pale green line graph, and a highlighted blue bar reading 1 KfW Germany with a German flag. Below it is a navy text box with white body copy. Column 2 (Environmental Finance): a large teal and green star logo reading Environmental Finance, Sustainable Debt Awards 2024, Winner, with the caption Green bond of the year – agency; below and to the right are two smaller star logos: Bond Awards 2023 Winner and Bond Awards 2022 Winner, each captioned Green bond of the year – agency. Column 3 (CMD-Capital Markets Daily): four teal laurel-wreath badges in a pyramid, each with 2026, a small title, KFW and cmdportal: top Best SSA Issuer; middle row left Best ESG Investor Relations Team, right Most Innovative Issuer; bottom Best GBP Issuer. Column 4 (GlobalCapital): a tall dark navy Bond Awards 2026 poster with a red neon WINNER, KfW, and three award lines; below it a wide dark navy MTN Awards 2026 banner with a glowing teal diamond-shaped KFW emblem on the right and a neon WINNER word on the left. All images are boxed, not cropped, sitting on white panels. The small 2x2 pixel image objects in the file are invisible placeholders and show nothing.

**Design**

Background navy #00446E. Title white #FFFFFF, bold humanist sans-serif (close to Frutiger or Meta), about 5.6% of slide height, sentence case. Column headings navy #00446E, semibold sans, about 3.2% of slide height. Quotes navy #00446E, semibold sans, about 3.6% of slide height. Body text white #FFFFFF regular sans, about 2.1% of slide height, on navy box. Column panels plain white #FFFFFF with square corners and no shadow, one panel at 97% opacity. Accents: teal #1A9AB0 and green #8DC63F from logos. Footer text white #FFFFFF, about 1.7% of slide height. Line and shape styles: flat rectangles, no borders, no gradients on panels.

### Slide 31

**Text**

Table of contents / Commitment to Sustainability

**Layout**

Full-bleed section divider with the title block set left-of-centre on the horizontal midline, starting at about 5.7% from the left edge and ending near 70% of the width. A small 'Table of contents' label sits in the top-right corner, right-aligned to about 94% of the width and about 6% from the top. The left third is flat, and the dotted pattern grows denser towards the right. No footer, slide number or logo is visible.

**Visuals**

Decorative background pattern only: a regular grid of small square dots in two tones of blue, arranged as a halftone or checkerboard. The dots are sparse and faint at the left, become denser and more contrasted towards the right edge, and are slightly lighter than the flat field. No photographs, charts, icons or logos.

**Design**

Background: Dark blue #00446E at left shading to #005F8E at right, with a square-dot halftone pattern across the whole slide. Flat dark-to-mid blue background with a square-dot halftone pattern fading in from left to right; no gradient overlay, shadows or rounded corners. The title and the top-right label are pale mint green. Typeface is a bold humanist sans-serif, close to Open Sans Bold or Fira Sans Bold, with no italics, no capitals and normal letter-spacing. The title is roughly 9% of slide height and the label roughly 4% of slide height. Colours: background #00446E at left, #005F8E at right; title and label #B8F5B0 (pale mint green); dot pattern #003E66 and #0A6496.

- illustration at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#00446E]: Full-slide dot-pattern background image: blue halftone grid of small squares with a left-to-right density gradient (Full-bleed, not cropped, no overlay; dots denser and more contrasted toward the right; dot tones #003E66 and #0A6496.)

### Slide 32

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: Mapping new financing against the 17 SDG icons with euro amounts ties the financial story to impact in a visually concrete way.

**Text**

SDG mapping of KfW Group’s new business / A contribution to all of the 17 United Nations’ Sustainable Development Goals / SDG contributions as evidence of our promotional mandate / Our promotional and financing activities are systematically mapped against the UN Sustainable Development Goals (SDGs). / KfW reports on its SDG contributions annually in a dedicated SDG Report. / In 2025, 97.1% of new financing commitments are aligned with at least one SDG. / KfW’s largest contributions relate to: / SDG 7 – Affordable and Clean Energy €44bn / SDG 8 – Decent Work and Economic Growth €23bn / SDG 11 – Sustainable Cities and Communities €34bn / SDG 13 – Climate Action €37bn / Shown are KfW Group’s financing contributions to the SDGs in 2025. / Source: About-KfW/Sustainability/Strategie-Management/KfW-und-SDG/ / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 32 / KfW

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white slide: a radial SDG graphic occupies the left column (about 3% to 48% of the width) and a light-blue information panel fills the right column (about 49% to 98%). The title and subtitle are left-aligned at the top margin (about 2.7% from the left edge), with the panel top aligned to the 20% line. The KfW logo sits bottom-left with the footer line beside it, and the page number is bottom-right at about 96% of the width; the source note is right-aligned inside the bottom of the panel.

**Visuals**

Left column: a radial diagram. An outer ring of 17 square SDG icons, each about 6% to 9% of slide width, placed clockwise from the top with its number and name: 1 No Poverty (red #E5243B), 2 Zero Hunger (mustard #DDA63A), 3 Good Health and Well-being (green #4C9F38), 4 Quality Education (dark red #C5192D), 5 Gender Equality (red-orange #FF3A21), 6 Clean Water and Sanitation (cyan #26BDE2), 7 Affordable and Clean Energy (yellow #FCC30B), 8 Decent Work and Economic Growth (maroon #A21942), 9 Industry, Innovation and Infrastructure (orange #FD6925), 10 Reduced Inequalities (magenta #DD1367), 11 Sustainable Cities and Communities (orange-yellow #FD9D24), 12 Responsible Consumption and Production (ochre #BF8B2E), 13 Climate Action (dark green #3F7E44), 14 Life Below Water (blue #0A97D9), 15 Life on Land (green #56C02B), 16 Peace, Justice and Strong Institutions (dark blue #00689D), 17 Partnerships for the Goals (navy #19486A). Inside the ring is a sunburst of segmented curved bars in the same goal colours, arranged in several concentric rings and radiating toward the icons; segment lengths vary and no values are printed, so no data is read from them. At the centre, a white circle carries the blue KfW wordmark (#005A8C) above the text 2025 (#005A8C). Right column: no charts, photographs or icons beyond the logo.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style on white. Titles and body text are dark blue #005A8C; the panel header band is light blue #C1E2F4 with the same dark blue text, and the panel body is pale blue #EAF5FB. The typeface is a humanist sans-serif in the Frutiger family or the closest common equivalent (regular for body, bold for the title and panel header, bold for the emphasised figures 97.1% and annually). Title cap height is about 4% of slide height (em about 5.0%); subtitle em about 3.6%; panel body em about 2.8%; panel header em about 3.0% bold; footnote and source em about 1.5%; footer em about 1.7%. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients; the panel has no border.

### Slide 33

**Text**

Sustainability and transparency at the forefront of KfW’s agenda / Selected impact information and stories / A strong commitment to transparency regarding the anticipated outcomes of our financing and promotional activities: / KfW’s "Impact Insights" provide valuable analyses of the outcomes from its new business activities in the areas of strengthening competitiveness, protecting climate and environment and securing livelihoods using ten selected indicators to highlight the positive contributions to the SDGs. / Full Report: / Link / KfW Impact Insights Financial Year 2025 / The impact of our financing and promotional activities / Bank aus Verantwortung / KFW / The wild – biodiversity / 14 LIFE BELOW WATER / 15 LIFE ON LAND / With total funding of EUR 51 million The wild is one of 306 biodiversity projects across more than 70 countries and cross-border regions. / Angola / Zambia / Namibia / Zimbabwe / Botswana / Africa / KaZa / The world‘s largest transboundary conservation area (52 million hectares), the Kovango Zambezi Transfrontier Conservation Area (KaZa), spans parts of Angola, Botswana, Namibia, Zambia and Zimbabwe. By reconnecting ecosystems across national borders, KaZa enables wildlife to once again follow their natural migration routes and improves protection against poaching. / Source & full story / The wild / Regional Challenge Fund / 4 QUALITY EDUCATION / 8 DECENT WORK AND ECONOMIC GROWTH / The economic landscape of the Western Balkan region is shaped by high youth unemployment and a shortage of skilled workers. / In the area of securing livelihoods KfW Development Bank on behalf of the German government financed the Regional Challenge Fund (RCF) with a total fund volume of EUR 73.4 million with the goal to support practical training and further education programs in cooperation with German and European companies. / Each year, approximately 5,400 young people are expected to be trained in the participating companies and vocational schools. The RCF thus contributes to strengthening the region as well as the German and European economies. / Source & full story / RCF Western Balkans / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 33

**Layout**

Title block spans the top across the full width, with a bold title line above a regular-weight subtitle line, left-aligned at about 2.7% from the left edge. Below, three columns of equal height sit on a common top edge near 18.4% and bottom edge near 88.8%: a dark blue text panel at left (x 2.4 to 34.1) and two tinted panels (green, then lavender) at centre and right, each with a coloured header bar and tinted body. A footer row sits at the bottom with the KfW wordmark at left, the footer text beside it, and the page number 33 at the bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three photographs and one map are placed in the columns. (1) Map at x 36.3 y 38.1 w 12.7 h 20.0 (322 by 285 px): pale grey-green stylised map of southern Africa with Angola, Zambia, Namibia, Botswana and Zimbabwe labelled in grey text; the KaZa conservation area is highlighted in bright yellow-green (#B5C400 about) in the centre; an inset of Africa at right in dark green (#2E6B4F about) with a small KaZa marker and a dotted leader line; a 'KaZa' wordmark in yellow-green at bottom left; the map sits on a near-white background and is boxed with no border. (2) Photograph at x 49.0 y 38.1 w 15.8 h 20.0 (370 by 263 px), full rectangle: several plains zebras standing and grazing on dry golden-brown grass with green shrubs and acacia-like trees behind; soft daylight; dominant colours olive green, tan and grey-white; uncropped, boxed. (3) Photograph at x 68.1 y 53.0 w 20.1 h 14.5 (472 by 191 px): interior of a workshop with two young men in blue work clothes operating a metal machine with a lathe-like device, grey and blue industrial equipment, bright overhead light; dominant colours grey, blue and beige. (4) Photograph at x 80.9 y 52.9 w 15.8 h 14.5 (369 by 191 px): smiling woman in a white headscarf and dark blue top with a patterned scarf, standing before a dark green chalkboard with faint writing and a blue door frame at right; dominant colours dark green, blue and white. (5) Tablet mockup at x 12.4 y 64.6 w 21.0 h 22.1 (665 by 392 px): black tablet, slightly tilted, showing a cover of a Hamburg harbour panorama at dusk with a blue-green water and buildings; a white card overlay reads 'KFW Impact Insights / Financial Year 2025 / The impact of our financing and promotional activities' in dark blue; 'KFW' logo in white at bottom right of the screen; the tablet is in front of the blue panel, uncropped. (6) Link icon at about x 4.5 y 75 w 6 h 8: white outline of a hand with pointing finger on a tablet/screen, with the word 'Link' in white handwriting-style text below it at about x 7 y 82 w 6 h 3. (7) SDG 14 icon at x 44.5 y 20.1 w 10.0 h 3.7 in the green header pair: two square tiles, left one blue (#0A97D9 about) with '14 LIFE BELOW WATER' and white fish and waves; right one green (#4C9F38 about) with '15 LIFE ON LAND' and white tree and ground lines. (8) SDG 4 and 8 icons at about x 81 y 20.1 w 14.3 h 3.7 in the lavender header: left tile red (#C5192D about) with '4 QUALITY EDUCATION' and white open book; right tile dark red (#A21942 about) with '8 DECENT WORK AND ECONOMIC GROWTH' and white rising bar chart with arrow. Divider lines: none.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF with three tinted column panels: blue #005A8C, green #B7F9AA at 10% opacity, lavender #7030A0 at 10% opacity. Clean white background with corporate blue and purple-lavender accents in a bright corporate style. Title text is dark blue #005A8C, bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger-like), about 5.3% of slide height. Subtitle is #005A8C regular weight about 3.4% of slide height. Body text in the blue panel is white #FFFFFF regular weight, about 2.2% of slide height, line spacing about 1.35. Body text in the green column is dark blue #005A8C regular, about 2.1%; bold runs in #005A8C bold. Body text in the lavender column is dark navy #0B2A4A regular with bold runs, about 2.1%. Column headers are bold sans, #005A8C, about 3.2%. Header bars: green #B7F9AA (full opacity) for the biodiversity column; lavender #C99AE1 for the Regional Challenge Fund column. Body panels: green #B7F9AA at 10% opacity and lavender #7030A0 at 10% opacity over white, giving pale tints. Blue panel #005A8C solid. Link text 'The wild' and 'RCF Western Balkans' are underlined #005A8C regular. Arrow glyphs are solid #005A8C right-pointing block arrows. Shapes are square-cornered with no shadows or gradients. Footer text is grey #6E6E6E regular about 1.6%. KfW wordmark in #005A8C bold.

### Slide 34

**Text**

Green Bonds – Made by KfW / KfW’s footprint in the Green Bond market / Eligible Project Categories / Renewable energies • Green buildings • / Clean transportation • / Biodiversity • Corporate investments in / climate mitigation / Liquidity / With large sizes in benchmark / maturities, KfW Green Bonds are / among the most liquid Green Bonds / in the market. / Contribution to SDGs / High Quality / Aligned with GB Principles & / Harmonized Framework for Impact / Reporting, SPO from Sustainalytics / Variety / KfW Green Bonds are available in / various currencies - in benchmark / size and non-vanilla, tailor-made / MTNs. / Credibility / Top ESG ratings and a strong focus / on green finance position KfW as / one of the most credible issuers of / green bonds. / Among Market Leaders / With over €100bn in 'Green Bonds – / Made by KfW' issued since 2014, / KfW is one of the largest issuers / globally. / Green Indices / Eligible for many green indices like / “The BofA Merrill Lynch GB Index”, / “Barclays MSCI GB Index”, “S&P GB / Index”, “Solactive GB Index”. / KfW / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 34

**Layout**

Two-column grid of eight colour tiles in four rows on the left half of the slide, with the title and subtitle stacked at top-left above the grid and a footer row at the bottom-left holding the KfW logo and footer text. A full-height photograph fills the right half, and the page number 34 sits at bottom-right over the photo; margins are about 2.7% on the left and about 4% at the top.

**Visuals**

Photograph on the right half (x 50.1 to 100, full height, full-bleed, not boxed): a white and black patterned tree nymph butterfly, Idea leuconoe, with folded wings, hangs from the pale green-yellow empty chrysalis case at upper centre-left, with its head and legs toward the top-centre. Composition is a vertical portrait crop with the butterfly occupying the centre, slightly right of middle. Soft natural daylight from the upper left, shallow depth of field, blurred grey-green and white bokeh background, dominant colours white, black, grey, pale green and olive. The photo has no overlay or tint. Six SDG icons sit in a row inside the Contribution to SDGs tile, each about 2.6% wide and 5.4% high with an approximate spacing of 0.5%: SDG 7 Affordable and Clean Energy (yellow #FCC30B tile with white sun-wheel icon), SDG 9 Industry, Innovation and Infrastructure (orange #FD6925 tile with white cube-structure icon), SDG 11 Sustainable Cities and Communities (orange #FD9D24 tile with white buildings icon), SDG 13 Climate Action (dark green #3F7E44 tile with white eye icon), SDG 14 Life Below Water (blue #0A97D9 tile with white fish and waves icon), SDG 15 Life on Land (green #56C02B tile with white tree and ground icon). KfW logo at bottom-left is a bold blue #005A8C wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF behind the left half, with the full-bleed photograph covering the right half. Plain white #FFFFFF background. Title and subtitle in blue #005A8C. Tile titles in white on blue, grey and dark green tiles and in dark green #2F6B3F on the light green tiles. Body text white on blue, dark green and grey tiles and dark green #2F5F3F on light green tiles. Accent blue #005A8C. Typeface is a humanist sans-serif similar to FF Meta: title in bold, tile titles in bold, body in regular weight. Title height is about 5.7% of slide height, subtitle about 3.4%, tile titles about 3.2%, body about 2.0%, footer about 1.6%. Tiles are flat rectangles with square corners, no borders, no shadows, no gradients, with white gutters of about 1% between them.

### Slide 35

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: the long funding run (slides 19 to 27) repeats a dense chart-plus-table layout with tiny table text and rotated bond labels, slide 15 is crowded, and slide 35 has a broken footer and slide 50 a jumbled chart legend.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the small table text and rotated x-axis labels on slides 19 to 27 and 50, clean up the footer on slide 35 and the legend on slide 50, and thin the crowded slide 15.

**Text**

Green Bond Framework 4.0
Commitment to Sustainability
RENEWABLE ENERGY
GREEN BUILDINGS
CLEAN TRANSPORTATION
BIODIVERSITY
CORPORATE INVESTMENTS FOR CLIMATE CHANGE MITIGATION
Wind energy (onshore & offshore)
Photovoltaic panels
Hydropower
Biogas energy
Grids and plants for the storage of heat or power
Domestic loan programme, international co-operation & project & export finance
Construction of new climate-friendly buildings with EH/EG 40 energy standard & CO2 budget over life cycle
Renovation of existing buildings which achieve the energy standard of an EH/ EG 85 or better
Domestic loan programmes
Climate-friendly vehicles for passenger transport and light commercial vehicles
Climate friendly vehicles for freight transport
Infrastructure for public transport
Infrastructure for active mobility
Domestic loan programmes, international co-operation & project & export finance
Financings or co-financings seeking to protect, maintain, restore or enhance biodiversity and ecosystems
Forest restoration, agroforestry, climate adaptation in arboreal ecosystems, education of local population
International co-operation projects
Production of climate-friendly technologies and products
Climate-friendly production processes to decarbonize production processes
Energy production from renewable sources for a company's own power consumption
Domestic loan programme
Investor presentation / November 2026
35

**Layout**

Title block sits top-left with the title on the first line and the subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned at about 2.7% from the left edge. A horizontal row of five chevron arrows runs across the upper-middle band, followed by five equal-width columns beneath, each with a coloured top bar and vertical rule. Photos and SDG badges form a bottom row aligned to the columns. A footer strip sits at the bottom with the logo at left, the footer text beside it and the page number at the far right.

**Visuals**

Five chevron-shaped banners in green, dark green, grey, light blue and dark blue, each with a circular icon at its left tip. The icons are, left to right: a white wind turbine on a pale green ring; white houses with a building on a pale green ring; a white electric car with a charging plug on a grey ring; white trees on a light blue ring; and a white solar panel with sun on a dark blue ring. Below each chevron is a column with bullet lists and a grey note. Under each column is a photograph in a rectangular box, and SDG badges overlay the lower right of the photos. Photo 1 shows offshore wind turbines in calm water under a pale blue sky. Photo 2 shows a white and red-roofed house among green trees under a blue sky. Photo 3 shows a yellow and black tram on a city street at sunset with overhead wires. Photo 4 shows two ring-tailed lemurs sitting on a branch in green foliage. Photo 5 shows a worker in a blue uniform installing blue photovoltaic panels in a factory. The photos are full rectangles with no crop shape, lit by natural daylight. The SDG badges are 7 Affordable and Clean Energy (yellow-orange, #F7B500), 13 Climate Action (green, #3F7E44), 11 Sustainable Cities and Communities (orange, #FD9D24), 14 Life Below Water (blue, #0A97D9), 15 Life on Land (green, #56A945), 9 Industry, Innovation and Infrastructure (orange-red, #F26A21). Each badge is a small square with a white icon and tiny white label.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold sans-serif in dark blue #005A8C at about 7% of slide height. Subtitle in regular sans-serif in dark blue #005A8C at about 4.3%. Body text in regular sans-serif in near-black #1A1A1A at about 2.6% of slide height, with bullets. Grey captions in regular sans-serif in #7F7F7F at about 2.3%. Chevron labels in bold uppercase white sans-serif at about 2.4%. Column accent colours: #23B75F, #398357, #A1ADB5, #54B3E2, #005A8C. Shapes are flat with square corners and no shadows or gradients. Icon rings are semi-transparent white over the chevron colour.

### Slide 36

**Text**

Allocation of KfW Green Bonds 2025 / Underlying Assets / Substantial Contribution Criteria (EU Taxonomy) / Clean transportation 3.0% / Biodiversity 0.8% / Europe 1.6% / Corporate Investments 7.6% / Non-Europe 1.2% / Other renewable energy technologies 1.2% / Solar energy / 3.6% / Germany 97.2% / Wind energy / 12.0% / 59.7% / Residential buildings / Non-residential buildings / 12.1 / € 20.3bn / 2025 / Source: KfW Green Bond Impact & Allocation Report 2025 / Potentially not aligned with SCC / 2.1% / of which: / Other renewable energies: 1.2% / Biodiversity: 0.8% / Partially aligned with SCC / 35.7% / of which: / Climate friendly new buildings: 35.7% / Clean Transportation: 3.0% / Corporate Investments: 7.6% / Green Buildings / 36.1% / of which: / Refurbishment of existing buildings: 36.1% / Fully aligned with SCC / Renewable Energy / 15.6% / of which: / Wind: 12.0% / Solar: 3.6% / Storage: <1% / Overall alignment with the Substantial Contribution Criteria (SCC) / Figures may not add up due to rounding differences. / KfW aims to create transparency and trust in the effectiveness of its Green Bonds through regular reporting. Two separate reports provide information on allocation and impact. Since 2022, the allocation report has been reviewed by an independent auditor (limited assurance). / KfW / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / April 2026 / November 2026 / 36

**Layout**

Title sits top-left at about 2.7% from the left edge, above two equal side-by-side panels: a left white panel with a dark blue header bar (Underlying Assets) and a right mint panel with a light green header bar (Substantial Contribution Criteria). Each panel spans about 46% of slide width, with a full-width pale blue banner of text below, then a footer row with the KfW logo at bottom left, footer text beside it and the page number at bottom right. Content is left-aligned to a margin of about 2.7%.

**Visuals**

Two charts and two icons. Left: a double-ring donut chart titled € 20.3bn / 2025 in the centre. Outer ring (light green shades) shows Residential buildings 59.7% (bright green #55D46A), Non-residential buildings 12.1 (dark green #3A7D52), Wind energy 12.0% (pale green #B7F9AA), Solar energy 3.6% (mid green #4E9A5F), Other renewable energy technologies 1.2% (pale green), Corporate Investments 7.6% (dark green #2A6046), Clean transportation 3.0% (olive #4E5A1E), Biodiversity 0.8% (dark olive). Inner ring (navy #005A8C) shows Germany 97.2%, with Europe 1.6% and Non-Europe 1.2% as thin slices in light blue and mid blue. Leader lines are drawn in green and grey to each label. Right: a single stacked vertical bar with segments from top to bottom: 2.1% light blue #8CC8F0 thin top slice, 35.7% navy #005A8C, Clean Transportation 3.0% dark green #266446, Corporate Investments 7.6% bright green #2DB843, Green Buildings 36.1% light green #94EB90, Renewable Energy 15.6% dark green #266446. Dashed guide lines run from the bar to the labels, and curly brackets on the left group the segments as Potentially not aligned, Partially aligned and Fully aligned with SCC. A bracket at the bottom points to the caption Overall alignment with the Substantial Contribution Criteria (SCC). Icon top right of left header: a white hand holding a sprout. Icon top right of right header: a dark green tree and a round green bush.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate look on white with pale tints for panels. Title in bold sans-serif (Frutiger-like, closest Open Sans Bold) in dark blue #005A8C, about 5.7% of slide height. Panel header text bold white on blue or bold dark green #266446 on mint, about 3.0%. Body and label text regular sans-serif in dark grey #444444 or green/blue matching their groups, about 1.8% to 2.2%. Accents: navy #005A8C, bright green #2DB843, pale green #B7F9AA, mint #ECFDED, light blue #EAF5FB, light blue text #4BA3DC for the Potentially not aligned label. Left panel border is a thin light blue line #A8D8F0 about 0.2% wide with square corners. Right panel border is a thin pale green line #B7F9AA. Square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Bottom banner has no border.

37

**Layout**

Full-width title block at the top left with the title on one line and a lighter subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned at about 2.7% margin. A chart occupies the left roughly two-thirds of the slide below the title, with a Highlights panel in the right third spanning from about 14% to 85% of slide height. A framework timeline bar sits under the chart x-axis, followed by footnotes, a footer line with the KfW logo at bottom left and the page number 37 at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Combined chart. Bars: 2014 to 2018 are single light green bars (renewable energy) with heights about 115, 155, 115, 155 and 40 on the left scale (estimates). From 2019 to 2025 bars are stacked: light green Renewable Energy at the base, dark blue Energy Efficiency/Green Buildings above it, with thin Clean Transportation (#007ABC) and Corporate Investments (light blue) slices at top. Total bar heights about 340 (2019), 350 (2020), 500 (2021), 450 (2022), 575 (2023), 590 (2024), 580 (2025) on the left scale (estimates). Printed percentages inside/above bars give shares: 2019 79% energy efficiency; 2020 79%; 2021 85%; 2022 2% (top segment) and 71%; 2023 1% and 71%; 2024 1% top, 70%, with 3% and 2% beside the bar; 2025 1% top, 8% and 3% beside, 72% in the main segment. Printed bar labels 135 (2019), 157 (2020), 136 (2021), 218 (2022), 195 (2023), 99 (2024), 126 (2025) sit on a light green line with markers, Green bond net proceeds (€ in billions – right scale), with points about 3 to 5 units on the right scale (estimates). Dark green line with markers, cumulated GHG reduced/avoided (million tons CO2e p.a., right scale 0 to 25), printed values 12.8 at 2018, 17.5 at 2021 and 25.2 at 2025; other points about 2014 2, rising steadily (estimates). Second light green line with markers, GHG reduced/avoided per €1m (tons CO2e p.a., left scale 0 to 1,000): about 880 (2014), 860 (2015), 790 (2016), 955 (2017), 980 (2018) (estimates), with the series continuing on the same line. Left axis 0 to 1,000 with gridlines at 250, 500, 750, 1,000; right axis 0 to 25 with ticks at 5, 10, 15, 20, 25. Gridlines are light grey horizontal lines. Legend in the upper left of the plot: Biodiversity (dark green square), Corporate Investments (light blue), Clean Transportation (#007ABC), Energy Efficiency/Green Buildings (#005A8C), Renewable Energy (light green #B7F9AA). Highlighted points: the 2025 point of the cumulated line labelled 25.2 and the 2021 point labelled 17.5. Framework bar beneath the axis: grey Framework label cell, then segments 2014 (dark blue #005A8C), 2019 (light blue #54B3E2), 2022 (grey), 2024 (light green #B7F9AA). Icons in the Highlights panel: a line-chart with magnifier icon and a pencil-on-document icon, both dark blue.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate investor style on white. Title bold humanist sans-serif (close to Fira Sans Bold) in #005A8C navy blue, about 6.0% of slide height. Subtitle regular weight same family, #005A8C, about 3.5%. Body text in the Highlights panel regular sans-serif in dark grey #41484C, about 2.6%. Chart labels in #41484C and #005A8C. Accent colours: #005A8C, #007ABC, #54B3E2, light blue #8ECDEF, light green #B7F9AA, dark green line #1E6B3A. Highlights header is a solid navy bar with white bold text. Panel fill light blue #E9F5FB with no visible border. Sharp square corners, no shadows, no gradients. Footnotes in small regular sans-serif about 1.3% of height, grey #41484C.

### Slide 39

**Text**

Learning objectives for 2026: standardisation & scalability
KfW’s role as an enabler and thought leader – both on the issuance and investment side
New issuance of KfW’s third DLT-based bond – this time with a focus on:
– Payments: leveraging the ECB’s DLT payment solution (first step: Pontes)
– Interoperability: proving the robustness of DLT
– Secondary market liquidity: strengthening liquidity by making use of the EU DLT Pilot Regime
Strengthening Germany’s and Europe’s financial centre & advancing Europe’s digital sovereignty
“Standardisation” & front-to-end digitalisation,
e.g., making use of smart contracts throughout the entire lifecycle of a security
Further testing of the German Electronic Securities Act (eWpG) and subsequently sharing insights with
the market, e.g., via whitepapers and lessons learned documents
KfW
Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
39

**Layout**

Four stacked full-width horizontal bands, each with a circular icon badge on its left edge, in a single left-aligned column spanning about 2.7% to 97.5% of the slide width. The title sits top-left at about 2.7% from the left and 3% from the top, with a one-line subtitle beneath it; text is left-aligned throughout. The KfW logo sits bottom-left and the footer text runs beside it, with the page number bottom-right at about 96% from the left.

**Visuals**

Four horizontal rounded bands and four matching dark navy circular icon badges, plus the KfW wordmark logo at bottom left. Badge 1 (top, #00446E circle, white outline ring) contains a white rocket icon; it sits beside the dark blue band. Badge 2 (#00446E) contains a white handshake icon beside the light green band. Badge 3 (#00446E) contains a white circular-arrows-with-gears icon beside the dark green band. Badge 4 (#00446E) contains a white graduation cap icon beside the light blue band. Each badge is about 6% of slide width and 10.7% of slide height, with a thin white ring separating it from the band. The bands are flat with rounded right corners (about 1% radius) and no shadows. The top band has a squared left edge where it meets its badge. Nothing is shown as a chart, table or photograph.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title and subtitle in medium-blue #005A8C. Title is a bold humanist sans-serif (close to Frutiger Bold), about 5.5% of slide height; subtitle is a regular-weight sans-serif (close to Frutiger Roman), about 3.3% of slide height. Band 1 #005A8C with white body text in regular sans-serif about 3.0% of slide height. Band 2 #B7F9AA with dark green text #2E5E3A. Band 3 #398357 with white text. Band 4 #97CFED with dark grey text #3A3A3A. Body text is regular weight. Bands use no gradients or shadows; corners rounded on the right side only. Footer text is grey #5A5A5A at about 1.9% of slide height. Page number is grey #5A5A5A.

### Slide 40

**Text**

The Digital Learning Journey of KfW
German Electronic Securities Act (eWpG) from 2021 as a catalyst for KfW’s learning journey
Dec 2022
Issuance of the / first¹ digital / bond as a / central register / security via / Clearstream / D7
Jul 2024
First / DLT-based / bond / (MVP 1.0)
Aug 2024
Second / DLT-based / bond / (MVP 2.0)
Feb 2025
Analysis paper on / market maturity of / DLT-based capital / market / with KPMG
Apr 2025
First² / investment / in DLT-based / covered bond / by Berlin Hyp / (on secondary / market)
May 2025
First³ CHF digital / bond within the / framework of the / Swiss National / Bank’s / “Project Helvetia”
Feb 2026
KfW participates / as investor in / Project Sovereign / (simulation of a / Greek digital / sovereign bond)
Mar 2026
KfW invests in / DZ BANK DLT- / based bond – / entire lifecycle / processed on / DLT
June 2026
KfW issued third / DLT-based bond as / a crypto security / and invests in / Saxony-Anhalt’s / First Blockchain- / Based Crypto / Security
July 2026
Start of European / blockchain initiative / RL1, with KfW as a co- / initiator
¹ Current outstanding volume of central register securities (eWpG): EUR 43.5 bn / ² Second investment: DLT-based bond by NRW.BANK (KfW as anchor investor in the primary market); Third investment: DLT-based covered bond by DekaBank / ³ Further CHF digital bonds were issued in September 2025, January 2026 and February 2026 – all within the framework of Project Helvetia
Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
40

**Layout**

Full-width 16:9 white slide with the title and subtitle left-aligned at the top-left margin (about 2.7% from the left edge). A single horizontal grey timeline runs across the middle (about 46% from top) with an arrowhead at the right; ten milestones alternate above and below it in a left-to-right sequence, each with a coloured circle icon, a dot on the axis, a bold date label and centred body text. Footnotes sit lower left above the footer; the KfW logo sits bottom-left and the footer line and page number sit bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Timeline diagram with ten milestones, left to right. Each milestone has a large coloured circle (about 6.5% wide) holding a white or tinted icon, a thin vertical grey connector line from the circle to a small dot on the horizontal axis, and a text label. Milestones 1, 3, 5, 7 and 9 (Dec 2022, Aug 2024, Apr 2025, Feb 2026, June 2026) have circles above the axis; milestones 2, 4, 6, 8 and 10 (Jul 2024, Feb 2025, May 2025, Mar 2026, July 2026) have circles below the axis. Icons, left to right: globe with laptop (light blue circle); rocket (dark blue circle); euro coin (light green circle); graduation cap (green circle); puzzle pieces (grey circle); shield with euro badge (blue circle); lightbulb with network lines (dark green circle); rocket (navy circle); shield with euro badge (blue circle); puzzle pieces (grey circle). One photograph: a small booklet cover, slightly rotated, with a violet-to-blue gradient abstract illustration, a white heading block reading DLT-basierter Kapitalmarkt, a small KfW logo at top right and a row of partner logos along the bottom; it overlaps the lower part of the graduation-cap circle. Two-pixel placeholder images sit under the title, subtitle, footer and logo areas and show no visible content.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 42

**Text**

The Germany Fund
KfW: a driving force and key contributor to Germany’s investment offensive
Successful start across all / target groups with ongoing progress:
The Germany Fund is not a traditional investment fund, but rather an umbrella structure for various target groups. Guarantees from the Federal Republic of Germany — over EUR 30 billion — enable the use of different KfW instruments that mobilise private capital and create a funding framework of approximately EUR 130 billion.
1 Core instrument:
Guarantees
≈EUR 30bn
In addition, budget funds and KfW’s own funds are used
Investments
≈EUR130bn
2 Target groups:
Start-ups/scale-ups
Industry/SMEs
Energy supply companies
Deutschlandfonds GROW WITH GERMANY
3 Tools:
Guarantees
Loans
Equity
Securitisation
Funds
4 Investment fields (excerpt):
Power grids
Raw materials
ClimateTech
Artificial intelligence
Safety and defence
Technology
Industry and SMEs
Hedging instrument for / transformation industries / Raw material fund / ABS/ABCP securitisations / SRT securitisations / SME Capital
Energy supply companies
Promotional loans geothermal energy / Investment loans and syndicated loans / “Renewable Energies Plus” incl. liability / exemption / Mezzanine capital for energy supply / Equity strengthening instruments
Start-ups and scale-ups
Direct co-investments in German tech / champions / WIN400 Seed Capital / First steps to scale Zukunftsfonds: tap / of tech fund investments / FOAK: First-of-a-kind loans / VC investments defense industry
Source and further information: https://www.kfw.de/About-KfW/Newsroom/Latest-News/Deutschlandfonds.html
Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
42

**Layout**

Two-column layout on a white field with a full-height dark blue panel occupying the right 27.7% of the slide. The title sits top-left at about 3.3% from the top and the subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned to a left margin of about 2.9%. The left area holds an intro paragraph at upper left, a central three-part diagram with a core-instrument block at left, a tools legend at right, and an investment-fields row at the bottom; three photos are stacked in a column at the left edge of the blue panel with their headings and bullet lists to their right. The source line sits at about 90% height, with the KfW logo and footer at the bottom left and the page number at bottom right of the blue panel.

**Visuals**

Central illustration: a stylised 'W' formed by three bold strokes (black at left, red in the middle, yellow/orange at right) overlaid on a light grey silhouette of France. Callout labels on black boxes with white text mark the target groups. A worker in a white hard hat and hi-vis vest with folded arms stands at the left, a red industrial robot arm rises from the top centre, and a yellow outline factory with chimneys and a lightning-bolt symbol and a pylon stands at the lower right. Left block: a shield icon with a check mark (Guarantees) and a coin stack icon (Investments), both in black outline. Right legend: outline icons for Guarantees (shield with check), Loans (document with euro sign), Equity (coin stacks), Securitisation (document with euro sign and bars), Funds (circular euro coin with arrows). Bottom row: six black outline icons for Power grids (pylon), Raw materials (log stack), ClimateTech (globe with leaf and gear), Artificial intelligence (sparkle in square), Safety and defence (worker with hard hat), Technology (gear with circuit lines). Right column: three photographs. Top photo shows a worker in a blue jacket and hard hat with machinery and a metal drum, warm yellow and brown tones, boxed. Middle photo shows yellow gas pipelines in the foreground and industrial tanks and a tower under a pale blue sky, boxed. Bottom photo shows a white robotic arm with a metal gripper against a light grey background, boxed. Shapes: a thin vertical dark blue line at about x 69.2% from y 25% to y 89%, with a dark blue open chevron pointing right at about y 55% that touches it. Logos: the GROW WITH GERMANY logo (black 'GROW' with a red and yellow stylised W and 'Deutschlandfonds' above and 'WITH·GERMANY' below) at upper right of the centre, and the KfW wordmark in dark blue at bottom left.

**Design**

Background white #FFFFFF with a full-height right panel in dark blue #00446E. Title in bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger-like) in dark blue #0B5C8F, about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle in regular weight of the same family in #0B5C8F, about 3.4%. Panel heading and tagline text in light green #A8E6A1, bold for the three section headings, about 2.6%. Panel bullet lists in white #FFFFFF regular sans, about 2.0%. Intro paragraph in dark grey #231F20 regular sans, justified, about 1.8%. Diagram labels in black #000000 bold condensed sans on black #000000 label boxes with white text. Footer and source text in dark grey #4D4D4D, about 1.6%. Line and shape styles: thin dark blue #00446E vertical rule and chevron with about 0.3% stroke. Photos are square-cornered with no shadow. No gradients or shadows.

### Slide 43

**Text**

The Germany Fund: Selected projects / KfW as a public-sector anchor for financing & risk sharing / Industry and SMEs with a focus on global markets / Start-ups and scale-ups / ABS securitisation / Transaction: abc finance, June 2026 / KfW’s role: EUR 70m investment in class A1 notes of a EUR 500m ABS asset pool of SME lease receivables / Originator: German leasing company abc finance GmbH / Anchor guarantor: European Investment Fund (EIF) / Impact: Unlocking fresh financial capacity for SME leases of over EUR 380m / Raw materials fund / Transaction: Lionheart, Q4 2025 / KfW’s role: Investing up to EUR 150m via the KfW Raw Materials Fund in the implementation of the project Lionheart / Investee: Vulcan Energy as project originator / Impact: KfW’s investment strengthens the supply of lithium for the production of batteries for electric vehicles by extracting lithium from geothermal brine sources in Germany, thereby contributing to improved supply security for Germany and Europe / Scale-up Direct / Transaction: Quantum Systems, Q4 2025 / KfW’s role: Equity co-investment by KfW’s VC arm KfW Capital / Investee: Quantum Systems is a German specialised unmanned aerial systems company, latest valuation at EUR 7bn (July 2026) / Impact: KfW-backed structuring improves investor confidence and helps mobilizing additional follow-on capital / Sources and further information: / https://www.kfw.de/About-KfW/Newsroom/Latest-News/News-Details_901888.html / https://www.kfw.de/About-KfW/Newsroom/Latest-News/Deutschlandfonds.html / https://www.kfw.de/stories/quantum_systems-2.html / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 43

**Layout**

Three equal columns of text and images sit below a full-width title block; the title is top-left at about 2.7% margin, with the subtitle beneath it and column headings at about 19% to 23% of slide height. Each column has a bold green heading, a smaller green subheading, and bulleted body text; a row of three equal photo panels (the third split into a logo panel and a photo) sits at 63.7% to 81.7% height, with source captions beneath. The footer holds the KfW logo at bottom-left, a footer line beside it, and the page number 43 at bottom-right, all on one baseline near 95% height.

**Visuals**

Three photographs and one logo panel in a row. (1) Left photo, EU flags: several blue flags with twelve yellow stars each hang on silver poles in front of a pale glass-and-steel building under daylight; the flags fill the frame edge to edge, boxed rectangle with square corners, no overlay; dominant colours royal blue about #1F4FB0, yellow #F7C92B, building grey-white. (2) Centre photo, Vulcan Energy: a white facade sign with blue capitals 'VULCAN ENERGY' and a teal-outlined block 'Lithium Electrolysis Frankfurt' on a light blue sky at top, a small blue-and-white logo mark to the left of the words, shot at a slight diagonal, boxed, no overlay; dominant colours white, blue #1B5FA8, teal #2FC7C0. (3) Right: a dark charcoal panel (about #2A2A2A) carrying the white 'QUANTUM SYSTEMS' wordmark in angular capitals, and beside it a photograph of a grey-white fixed-wing unmanned aircraft with propeller nose and tail number 1312 on the fuselage, parked on a hangar floor with reflections, boxed, no overlay; dominant colours light grey #C8CCD0, dark grey #3A3F44. No charts or tables.

**Design**

Background: Solid colour #00446E (deep navy-teal) covering the whole slide.

### Slide 44

**Text**

Table of contents
Appendix
Credit and Business Model

**Layout**

Full-bleed section divider with no grid. The section label "Table of contents" sits top right, right-aligned to about 95% of the width and about 6% from the top. The title block is left-aligned at about 5.7% from the left edge, vertically in the middle-to-lower half, with "Appendix" in bold above "Credit and Business Model" in regular weight. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed background halftone pattern: a grid of small square dots in a slightly darker green than the base, fading from almost invisible at the left (about 0 to 30% of width) to dense and more saturated at the right edge. No photographs, illustrations, charts, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is a pale mint-green base with a green square-dot halftone gradient increasing toward the right. All text is dark cerulean blue. "Table of contents" and "Appendix" are bold humanist sans-serif, and "Credit and Business Model" is a regular-weight sans-serif of the same family. Title size is about 8.9% of slide height (cap height about 6% of height), subtitle about 8.3%, and the section label about 4.4%. Lines are not used. No shapes, corner radii, shadows or outlines are visible.

**Visuals**

Choropleth-style world map (vector, not a photograph) in a flat, borderless projection showing countries as filled shapes in three tones: light blue #B9D8E7 for most countries and oceans-adjacent land, mid blue-grey #7199A9 for a selected set of countries, and bright blue #55A0C6 for the same selected set in a lighter variant overlay. Country borders are thin white lines. Office locations are about 80 small dark blue #00598C dots (roughly 0.5% of slide width each), each with a dark grey #222222 label placed beside it, some with short leader lines to clustered European and Balkan points (Bonn, Cologne, Berlin, London, Brussels, Frankfurt, Belgrad, Chisinau, Kyiv, Tbilisi, Pristina, Istanbul, Sarajevo, Podgorica, Tirana, Skopje, Ankara, Baku, Yerevan, Tashkent, Mazar-e-Sharif, Bishkek, Dushanbe, Ulan Bator, Beijing, Kabul, Islamabad, Kathmandu, New Delhi, Dhaka, Yangon, Bangkok, Hanoi, Vientiane, Phnom Penh, Ho Chi Minh City, Manila, Singapore, Jakarta, Mumbai, Abu Dhabi, Sanaa, Beirut, Amman, Ramallah-Al Bireh, Cairo, Rabat, Tunis, Dakar, Bamako, Niamey, Ouagadougou, Lomé, Cotonou, Accra, Yaoundé, Addis Abeba, Kampala, Kigali, Bujumbura, Nairobi, Kinshasa, Daressalam, Lusaka, Lilongwe, Windhoek, Pretoria, Maputo, Johannesburg, Mexiko City, Guatemala City, San Salvador, Tegucigalpa, Managua, New York, Bogotá, Quito, Lima, La Paz, Brasilia, São Paulo). **Layout**

Two columns on a 49/51 split: a full-height photograph fills the left 49% of the slide edge to edge, and the right column holds the text on a white field with a 6% left margin inside the column starting at 53%. The title is centred in the right column near the top, followed by a large centred figure, a subtitle, a green pointer rule, a green Strategy panel and a grey Asset classes panel stacked below. A small source line sits bottom left of the right column, and the page number sits bottom right at about 97% of the width. The KfW logo and footer line sit over the bottom-left of the photograph.

**Visuals**

One photograph occupying the left half, full-bleed to the top, bottom and left edges. Subject: a modern office complex in a German city, seen from an elevated street-level viewpoint. On the left is a tall cylindrical glass tower with blue-tinted vertical mullions and a blue KfW logo at its top. Behind and to the right is a taller rectangular office block with a blue KfW logo on its upper section and a stepped roof of grey louvres. In the centre-right foreground is a pale grey stone building with a glass staircase tower on its face. The glass tower shows a grid of coloured panels in red, orange, green, yellow and blue. Several dark cars and one small white car are on a wide multi-lane road with lane markings and arrows, bordered by trees on the left. Lighting is soft early-evening daylight with a pale sky gradient at the top. Dominant colours are blue-grey glass, warm grey stone, green foliage and pale beige sky. The image is not cropped in its main subject and is not tinted; a small KfW logo in white sits over the bottom left, with a white footer line over the road. A thin white diagonal-free area separates it from the right column. Also present: a green pointer triangle (downward-pointing chevron) under the subtitle, drawn in light green #B7F9AA as a thin line with a centred notch.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF on the right, with a full-bleed photograph covering the left 49% of the slide. White background. Title and key figure in KfW blue #005B8E, bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger-like). Subtitle in the same blue, regular weight. Body bullets in dark green #266446 inside the Strategy panel and in black #000000 inside the Asset classes panel, regular sans-serif. Strategy header bar fill #B7F9AA with bold dark green #266446 centred text. Strategy body fill #ECFDED. Asset classes header bar fill #6D767D with white bold centred text. Asset classes body fill #EEF0F2. Title size about 5.2% of slide height, big figure about 9.5%, subtitle about 2.5%, panel headings about 3.2%, bullets about 2.9%, footer and source about 1.8%. Shapes are plain rectangles with square corners and no shadows or gradients. The pointer rule is a light green #B7F9AA line about 0.3% of height thick. Photo has no border.

**Layout**

Three-column table layout on a white slide: a title and subtitle block top left, then three columns of tables under dark blue header bars (Strategic holdings at left, Mandated transactions in the centre, Privatization initiatives at right), with the footnote block bottom centre and the KfW logo, footer line and page number along the bottom. Titles and footer left-aligned to a left margin of about 2.7 percent of width; slide number right-aligned at about 97 percent; the Privatization column ends at about 96 percent.

**Visuals**

Table, no chart. Strategic holdings: 13 rows, columns Company and Share (capital share), with parent companies in dark-grey bold and subsidiaries indented on light green bands: DEG mbH 100% (parent); DEG Impact GmbH 100%; DEG Impulse gGmbH 100%; KfW Beteiligungsholding GmbH 100% (parent); KfW IPEX-Bank GmbH 100%; IPEX Asia Ltd. 100%; AKA Ausfuhrkredit GmbH 0.21%; KfW Capital GmbH & Co. KG 100%; tbg GmbH 100%; Berliner Energie-Agentur GmbH (BEA) 25%; True Sale International GmbH (TSI) 7.7%; FuB GmbH 100%; Interkonnektor GmbH 100%. Mandated transactions: columns Company and Share, rows HENSOLDT AG 25.1%; BioNTech SE 0.6%; GZBV Verwaltungs-GmbH 100% and GZBV mbH & Co. KG 84.3% in one cell row; Selent Netzbetreiber GmbH 100%; German LNG Terminal GmbH 50%; Expand Netzbetreiber GmbH 100%; Scopello GmbH 100%; European Investment Fund (EIF) 2.3%. Privatization initiatives: Deutsche Post AG 17.73%; Deutsche Telekom AG 14.20%. Highlighted cells: the DEG Impact, DEG Impulse, KfW IPEX-Bank and IPEX Asia rows in bright green #BCF0C5 with the pale green #DDF7E2 for IPEX Asia and AKA Ausfuhrkredit. No lines or borders; rows are separated by white gaps. Logo: KfW wordmark in dark blue bold sans-serif at bottom left, about 2.7 to 6.8 percent of width and 94.9 to 97.5 percent of height.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles in dark blue #00446E; body and table text dark grey #414042; subtitle in regular weight dark blue #00446E. Typeface is a humanist sans-serif such as Frutiger, bold for title and headers and medium for table text. Title cap height about 5.6 percent of slide height, subtitle about 3.4 percent, table text about 2.2 percent, footnotes about 1.05 percent. Header bars and text are white on #00446E. Table cells are flat rectangles filled #EBF3F7 with square corners and no borders or shadows. Green highlight bands #BCF0C5 and #DDF7E2. No gradients or shadows.

Single-column content slide. The title sits top-left at about x 2.7%, y 3% and spans roughly 56% of the width. A full-width-of-content table sits below it from x 2.7% to 91.9%, y 14.7% to 88.4%, with a left label column (x 2.7% to 52.5%) and two right-aligned numeric columns (x 52.5% to 72.0% and 72.0% to 91.9%). The footer runs along the bottom: KfW logo at left (x 2.7%, y 95.7%), footer text starting at x 8.3%, and page number '48' right-aligned near x 96%, y 96%. Left margin about 2.7%, right margin about 8%.

**Visuals**

One table. Columns: (1) row label, blank header; (2) 'H1/2026'; (3) 'H1/2025'. Rows: header row; 'Business activities (in EUR bn) – for the period' section row (no values); 'Promotional business volume' 57.7 | 39.4; 'Income statement key figures (in EUR bn) – for the period' section row; 'Operating result before valuation & promotional activities' 1.1 | 1.0; 'Consolidated profit' 0.9 | 0.3; 'Consolidated profit before IFRS effects from hedging' 0.9 | 0.5; 'Balance sheet (in EUR bn) – at the end of the period' section row with column values 30/06/2026 | 31/12/2025; 'Total assets' 550.4 | 540.7; 'Equity' 41.5 | 40.6; 'Volume of business' 730.2 | 706.4; 'Key regulatory figures (in %) – at the end of the period' section row; 'Tier 1 capital ratio' 28.1 | 28.4; 'Total capital ratio' 28.1 | 28.5. Thin white vertical dividers in header row; thin light-green horizontal row rules; outer border about 1 px in #266446. Logo: KfW wordmark in dark blue (#00598F). No photographs, icons or charts.

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger-like), dark blue #00598F, about 5.6% of slide height, left-aligned. Hidden text 'Ratings' is white bold Arial, about 2.5% of height, placed over the header near x 49.2%; it is not visibly rendered on the slide.

### Slide 49

**Text**

Table of contents
Appendix
Funding

**Layout**

Two-zone layout: the section title 'Appendix' over 'Funding' sits left-aligned at about 5.7% from the left edge, vertically centred near the middle of the slide. The running header 'Table of contents' is top-right, right-aligned to about 94% of the width, with its baseline near 11% of the height. There is no footer, no slide number and no logo; the right two-thirds of the slide is an empty patterned field.

**Visuals**

Non-text visual: a halftone pattern of small square dots in two slightly different greens, laid over the whole slide. The dots are almost invisible at the left edge and grow larger and more saturated toward the right and bottom-right corner, so the pattern reads as a left-to-right gradient of squares. No photograph, chart, icon or logo is present. The pattern is full-bleed and unframed.

**Design**

Background is a pale mint-green field (#D3F9CC) at the left that deepens to a brighter green (#B3F59B) at the right, with the square halftone dots overlaid. Title 'Appendix' is bold and 'Funding' is regular weight, both in a deep blue (#005A96). The header 'Table of contents' is semibold in the same deep blue (#005A96). Typeface is a humanist sans-serif similar to Fira Sans or Source Sans. The 'Appendix' cap height is about 6% of slide height, so the type size is about 9% of slide height; the header is about 4.2% of slide height. There are no shapes, borders, shadows or corner radii; all edges are square.

### Slide 50

**Review notes**

- Review: The weaknesses are consequential: the long funding run (slides 19 to 27) repeats a dense chart-plus-table layout with tiny table text and rotated bond labels, slide 15 is crowded, and slide 35 has a broken footer and slide 50 a jumbled chart legend.
- Suggested repair: Enlarge or cut the small table text and rotated x-axis labels on slides 19 to 27 and 50, clean up the footer on slide 35 and the legend on slide 50, and thin the crowded slide 15.

**Text**

KfW in NOK & SEK / Expanding green activities into Nordic region / NOK/SEK / Characteristics / KfW is one of the largest NOK and SEK issuer in the market / Offering in both markets some of the most liquid bonds / Flexibility in deal/re-opening size and maturities / Tailor-made issuance in DKK is possible / Highlights 2025 / One of the largest issuer in Nordic Currencies / KfW issued 2 new NOK lines (1 green) / KfW issued 2 new SEK lines / KfW issued 1 new green DKK line / 2026 / NOK bn / Green / Tenor / Coupon (%) / Lead Managers / June 29 / 2.0 / N / 5y / 4.480 / Danske / Re-openings / 2.7 / 2026 Total / 4.7 / 2026 / SEK bn / Green / Tenor / Coupon (%) / Lead Managers / Jan 13 / 1.25 / Y / 5y / 2.750 / Danske / 2026 Total / 1.25 / NOK bn / 14 / 12 / 10 / 8 / 6 / 4 / 2 / 0 / Tap 2026 / Tap Green / Tap / New Issue Green / New Issue 2026 / New Issue / KfW NOK 2027/02 / KfW NOK 2027/12 green / KfW NOK 2028/03 / KfW NOK 2028/09 / KfW NOK 2029/01 green / KfW NOK 2029/02 green / KfW NOK 2029/09 / KfW NOK 2030/01 / KfW NOK 2030/04 green / KfW NOK 2031/12 new / 2027 / 2028 / 2029 / 2030 / 2031 / SEK bn / 6 / 5 / 4 / 3 / 2 / 1 / 0 / KfW SEK 2028/10 green / KfW SEK 2028/10 / KfW SEK 2028/02 green / KfW SEK 2028/01 / KfW SEK 2029/12 green / KfW SEK 2029/08 / KfW SEK 2030/09 green / KfW SEK 2030/03 / KfW SEK 2031/10 new green / KfW SEK 2031/09 / 2028 / 2029 / 2030 / 2031 / KfW / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 50

**Layout**

Two-column layout below a full-width header: title block top-left with the subtitle beneath it, and a large NOK/SEK label top-right aligned to the right margin. A dark blue full-width bar carries the Characteristics heading; the left column (about 36% width) holds two stacked panels (Characteristics and Highlights 2025 with tables), and the right column (about 49% to 97% width) holds the NOK bar chart and the SEK bar chart side by side, with the flags above each. The KfW logo sits bottom-left with the footer line beside it and the page number '50' bottom-right, all on a common margin of about 2.6%.

**Visuals**

Two stacked column charts, plus two flags and two tables. (1) Left chart, NOK bn: stacked vertical bar chart, y-axis 0 to 14 in steps of 2 labelled NOK bn, x-axis grouped by year 2027, 2028, 2029, 2030, 2031. Bars from left to right, stacked bottom to top with values about: KfW NOK 2027/02: New Issue about 3, Tap about 0.5 (total about 3.5). KfW NOK 2027/12 green: New Issue Green about 1.5, Tap Green about 1.5 (total about 3). KfW NOK 2028/03: New Issue about 2, Tap 2026 about 0.7 (total about 2.7). KfW NOK 2028/09: New Issue 2, Tap about 10 (total 12). KfW NOK 2029/01 green: New Issue Green 3, Tap Green 3 (total 6). KfW NOK 2029/02 green: New Issue Green 2. KfW NOK 2029/09: New Issue 1. KfW NOK 2030/01: New Issue 2. KfW NOK 2030/04 green: New Issue Green 3, Tap Green 2 (total 5). KfW NOK 2031/12 new: New Issue 2026 2, Tap 2026 2 (total 4). Legend (top to bottom): Tap 2026 light purple, Tap Green checkered green and white, Tap light blue, New Issue Green green, New Issue 2026 purple, New Issue dark blue. Values are estimates (about) where no value is printed. No highlighted points. (2) Right chart, SEK bn: stacked vertical bar chart, y-axis 0 to 6 in steps of 1 labelled SEK bn, grouped by year 2028, 2029, 2030, 2031. Values about: KfW SEK 2028/10 green New Issue Green 2; KfW SEK 2028/10 New Issue 1.25; KfW SEK 2028/02 green New Issue Green 5; KfW SEK 2028/01 New Issue 1.25; KfW SEK 2029/12 green New Issue Green 2 with Tap Green 1 (total 3); KfW SEK 2029/08 New Issue 0.5; KfW SEK 2030/09 green New Issue Green 1; KfW SEK 2030/03 New Issue 1.25; KfW SEK 2031/10 new green New Issue Green 1.25; KfW SEK 2031/09 New Issue 1 with Tap 1 above (total 2). Series colours as in the NOK legend. (3) Flag of Norway (red field with white-edged navy cross) boxed above the NOK chart, top-right of the chart. (4) Flag of Sweden (blue field with yellow cross) boxed above the SEK chart. (5) Table 1, NOK: columns 2026, NOK bn, Green, Tenor, Coupon (%), Lead Managers; rows June 29 / 2.0 / N / 5y / 4.480 / Danske; Re-openings / 2.7 / blank / blank / blank / blank; 2026 Total (bold) / 4.7. (6) Table 2, SEK: columns 2026, SEK bn, Green, Tenor, Coupon (%), Lead Managers; row Jan 13 / 1.25 / Y / 5y / 2.750 / Danske; 2026 Total (bold) / 1.25. (7) KfW logo, bottom left, dark blue wordmark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Titles and accent elements in dark blue #005A8C. Body text dark grey #41484C. Header bars are solid #005A8C with white bold Arial-style sans-serif headings. Title 'KfW in NOK & SEK' is a bold humanist sans (close to Frutiger Bold) in dark blue, about 6% of slide height; subtitle regular weight about 3.4%; NOK/SEK large light-weight sans about 8.5%. Body bullets about 2.65% of slide height, Arial-like regular. Table text about 1.5%. Left panel fill #E9F5FB, no border, square corners. Chart gridlines light grey, axis labels grey #7E8A90. Flags boxed with no shadow. No gradients or shadows.

**Layout**

Full-width single chart on a white slide. Title is top-left at about 2.7% from the left edge and 3.5% from the top, with the subtitle beneath it; the chart fills the middle band from about 15% to 75% of the height. Annotations hang below the x-axis baseline at the left and right of the chart; footer row sits at the bottom with the KfW logo at left, footer text next to it and the page number at bottom right, all left-aligned to a margin of about 2.7%.

**Visuals**

Line chart, no title beyond the slide title, no visible axis lines, tick labels, gridlines or legend. X-axis runs from 1958 to 2025 with year labels printed under selected points only; Y-axis is unlabelled, with values printed as green data labels at selected points. Series: one dark blue (#005A8C) polyline, about 2.5 pt stroke, with a point every year. Labelled values: 1958 0.1; 1963 0.3; 1968 0.2; 1973 0.3; 1978 1.3; 1983 2.4; 1988 5.5; 1993 7.6; 1998 32.0; 2003 50.7; 2008 75.3; 2013 65.4; 2018 76.1; 2023 90.2; 2025 78.1. Unprinted points are about 0.1 to 80 on the same scale, estimated from the curve. Highlighted points: small solid green (#56D864) dots about 1 percent of slide width at each labelled year. Event callouts are dark blue vertical leader lines dropping from the curve to the annotation text, at 1958, 1987, 1998, 2001, 2014 and 2024. Chart is a single raster image spanning the plot area, with several tiny 2x2 pixel fragments that are part of the image rendering.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title in bold humanist sans-serif (close to Fira Sans Bold), dark blue #005A8C, about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle in regular weight sans-serif, dark blue #005A8C, about 3.2% of slide height. Data values in bold sans-serif, green #56D864, about 3.5% of slide height. Year labels in bold sans-serif, dark blue #005A8C, about 2.0% of slide height. Event annotations in bold sans-serif, dark blue #005A8C, about 2.0% of slide height, left- or right-aligned. Footer text in regular sans-serif, grey #6B6B6B, about 1.5% of slide height. Page number in regular sans-serif, grey #6B6B6B. Line and leader lines are flat, no shadows, no corner radii, no gradients. Some white rectangles sit behind chart labels to mask the curve.

### Slide 52

**Text**

Table of contents
Appendix
Sustainability

**Layout**

Section divider with no header bar, footer or slide number. The left-aligned title block sits in the left third, starting at about 5.7% from the left edge, with 'Appendix' in bold above 'Sustainability' in a light weight, both sitting just above the vertical middle. A small 'Table of contents' label is in the top-right corner at about 6% from the top, right-aligned to about 94% of the width.

**Visuals**

A full-bleed halftone pattern of small green squares fills the slide. The squares are densest and most saturated at the right and fade to almost nothing at the left, with a soft left-to-right gradient behind them. No photograph, chart, logo or icon is present. The text is set as image-based lettering in the file, not live text.

**Design**

Background is a pale mint green gradient, about #D4FAD0 on the left fading to about #B3F5A3 on the right, overlaid with a halftone grid of lighter and darker green squares (about #BDF7AE and #C9FAC4). All text is dark blue, about #00518C. 'Appendix' is a bold humanist sans-serif, roughly Fira Sans Bold or Source Sans Bold, about 9.5% of slide height. 'Sustainability' is the same family in a regular or light weight, about 8.3% of slide height. 'Table of contents' is a bold humanist sans, about 3.8% of slide height. Title text is sentence case with no capitals treatment and normal letter-spacing. No lines, borders, shadows or corner radii are present.

**Elements and positions** (back to front; x, y, width, height as % of the slide; type, colour and alignment in brackets)

- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#FFFFFF]: Solid white fill covering the whole slide, fully hidden behind the pattern image (Base layer only; no visible effect because the background pattern image covers it completely.)
- shape at x 0, y 0, w 100, h 100 [#B3F5A3]: Full-bleed halftone background pattern: small squares of light and mid green in a grid, fading in density from left to right over a mint gradient (Covers the entire slide edge to edge, not cropped or boxed, no overlay or tint beyond the pattern itself. Square cells roughly 1% of slide width, with lighter squares about #C9FAC4 and mid squares about #BDF7AE over the gradient.)

Sustainability in the Financial Markets department (KfW Treasury) / Holistic approach for all business counterparties / Financial Markets (KfW Treasury) / Responsible for: / ✓ Liquidity Management / ✓ Funding / ✓ Asset/Liability Management / Business segments / • Money market activities / Repo / • Derivatives / • Portfolio management (Liquidity portfolio) / • KfW bond issuances (incl. **Layout**

Two-column grid on a white field: a narrow left column (about 2.6% to 36.5% of width) holds three stacked panels, each a blue header bar over a pale-blue body, and a wider right area (about 39% to 97.6%) holds a full-width green banner over three equal columns of equal height. The title sits top-left at about 2.5% from the top with the subtitle beneath it, both left-aligned to x 2.7%. The footer runs along the bottom with the KfW logo at left, the footer text beside it and the page number 53 at right; all panels end at about 88% of the height.

**Visuals**

Three simple flat line-style icons in solid green (#3B8C5E) over the first three columns of the right area. (1) Above 'ESG Integration in internal Credit Assessments' at about x 46.5%, y 26.5%, w 3.3%, h 5.7%: a clipboard/checklist icon, a rectangle with a top clip and a large white check mark cut into it. (2) Above 'Counterparty Screening' at about x 65%, y 25%, w 3.3%, h 5.7%: a raised index finger with a small circle containing a white exclamation mark at its upper right. (3) Above 'Dialogue' at about x 82%, y 25%, w 6.5%, h 7%: two solid silhouettes of a woman (left) and a man (right) side by side, heads and shoulders, no detail. No charts, tables or photographs. The KfW logo at bottom left is the wordmark KfW in dark blue (#005A8C). No other logos.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate white background. Title and header bars in KfW dark blue #005A8C; subtitle in #005A8C regular weight; body text in dark slate grey #41484C; green banner in mint #B7F9AA with dark grey #41484C bold text; light blue body panels #E9F5FB; green column panels #ECFDED. Typeface is a humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger, the title in bold, the subtitle and body in regular, column headings in semibold. Title text is about 7% of slide height, subtitle about 3.8%, panel headers about 2.9% bold white, body about 2.4%, footer about 1.9%. Shapes are flat rectangles with square corners and no borders, no shadows, no gradients. Bullets are small filled dots in grey; checkmarks are Wingdings ticks in grey.

### Slide 54

**Text**

Independent opinion by Morningstar Sustainalytics
External analysis by renowned research institution
KfW Green Bond Framework 4.0 – applicable from January 2024 onwards
Morningstar Sustainalytics, a globally-recognized provider of ESG research, ratings and data, has
completed over 1000 Second Party Opinion (SPO) projects.
Sustainalytics is of the opinion that the Green Bonds – Made by KfW Framework is credible and impactful and aligns with the Green Bond Principles 2021.
Sustainalytics mapped the criteria defined in the Framework's use of proceeds categories to 78 activities in the EU Taxonomy. Sustainalytics is of the opinion that 63 activities align with the applicable significant contribution (SC) criteria, 7 partially align and 8 are not aligned.
Sustainalytics assessed the Framework and the alignment thereof with relevant industry standards including the EU Taxonomy and provided views on the robustness and credibility of the Framework.
Framework 2014 (assessed by CICERO)
Framework 2019 (assessed by CICERO)
Framework 2022 (assessed by CICERO)
✓ Shades of Green: dark green
✓ Governance: excellent
✓ In line with ICMA GBP (June 2018)
✓ Shades of Green: medium green
✓ Governance: excellent
✓ In line with ICMA GBP (June 2021)
✓ Shades of Green: medium green
✓ Governance: excellent
Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
54

**Layout**

Two-part vertical stack on a white field with 2.7% side margins. Title and grey-blue subtitle sit top-left above a full-width dark navy banner bar; below it a logo and lead sentence row, a light green divider with a centre V-notch, three icon-led paragraphs, then three equal-width coloured header boxes with checklists beneath them in three columns. The KfW logo sits bottom-left with the footer text beside it and the page number bottom-right, all on a shared baseline near 95% of the height.

**Visuals**

Icons: three circular icons aligned to the left of the three paragraphs. Icon 1 is a dark blue #005A8C filled circle (about 2.8% wide by 4.9% tall) with a white icon of a group of people with speech bubbles. Icon 2 is a light green #B7F9AA filled circle of the same size with a dark green hand-and-leaf icon. Icon 3 is a grey #6D767D filled circle of the same size with a white clipboard-with-checkmark icon. Logos: the Morningstar Sustainalytics logo (red wordmark #E31E24 'MORNINGSTAR' in dark grey with a thin vertical divider, and bold red 'SUSTAINALYTICS') at the left of the lead sentence, placed as a 340 by 55 pixel image; the KfW logo in dark blue #005A8C at bottom-left. Divider: a thin light green #B7F9AA horizontal line across the full content width with a V-shaped notch pointing down at horizontal centre. Table-like structure: three coloured header boxes (dark blue #005A8C, light green #B7F9AA, grey #6D767D) each followed by a checklist; no chart. No photographs.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background with no gradient or texture. Title colour #005A8C, subtitle colour #005A8C, banner fill #00446E with white text, body text near-black #1A1A1A, bullet and checklist text #41484C with green accents (dark green #2E7D4F for 'dark green', medium green #1E9E4F for 'medium green') and blue accent #005A8C for 'excellent'. Typeface appears to be a humanist sans-serif similar to Frutiger: title bold, subtitle regular, body regular, banner text semibold. Title text is about 5.5% of slide height, subtitle about 3.2%, body about 2.5%, footer about 1.7%. Lines and shapes are flat with no corner radii, shadows or gradients; the header boxes are sharp-cornered rectangles. Check marks are Wingdings ticks in #41484C.

### Slide 55

**Text**

Project evaluation and selection process
Domestic projects
KfW loans are extended to final borrowers via intermediaries / such as commercial banks or local saving banks.
Intermediaries apply their regular loan procedure, assume the / liability for repayment to KfW and screen against the specific / programmes’ eligibility criteria KfW defined.
In a 2nd step, the KfW lending department reviews the loan / application and compliance with the eligibility criteria of the / specific KfW loan programme.
All projects approved by KfW’s lending department for eligible / loan programmes immediately qualify for KfW Green Bonds / without further selection or approval process.
International financings
For financing provided by KfW in the context of international / cooperation and project and export finance, a selection / process has been set up for the identification and approval of / Eligible Green Projects in line with the criteria of KfW’s Green / Bond Framework.
The relevant business functions within KfW group are / responsible for selecting a list of potential eligible financings / that is then submitted to KfW’s Financial Markets Department.
Representatives from KfW’s financial markets as well as / representatives responsible for sustainability and finance from / the relevant business functions within KfW group will meet on / a regular basis to review proposed Eligible Green Projects and / validate the selection.
KfW
Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026
55
Bank
Lender
Financing partner
Final borrower
KFW

**Layout**

Two equal columns below a full-width title, separated by a thin vertical grey divider running from just under the column headers to about 91% of the slide height. The title sits top-left at about 2.7% margin; the process diagram sits in the top-right corner, above the right column. Each column has a full-width header bar (left dark blue, right light green) with its heading inset about 3% from the left edge. Footer with KfW logo at bottom left and the page number at bottom right, both at roughly 96% of slide height.

**Visuals**

1) Process diagram in top-right, boxed on white, about 27.6% wide and 20.1% tall: three grey building-style pictograms left to right connected by two dark blue double chevrons (›› shapes). Left: a tall grey office building with white window stripes and the blue label 'KFW' on its top, captioned by a dark blue rounded label 'Lender'. Centre: a grey stacked-storey building with a darker grey top block reading 'Bank' in black, captioned by a dark blue rounded label 'Financing partner'. Right: a green-olive house with three white windows, a small dark green tree at its left, blue-and-white solar panels on its right roof and a white wind turbine rising behind it, captioned by a dark blue rounded label 'Final borrower'. 2) Light blue (#D1EBF7) silhouette map of Germany, about 6% wide, overlapping the right edge of the dark blue Domestic projects bar at top-right of that bar. 3) Green world map (dark green #398357 continents, flat style, no border), about 5.9% wide and 6.8% tall, overlapping the right end of the light green International financings bar. 4) Thin vertical grey divider line, about 0.1% wide, from about y 23% to y 91%, centred at x 50%.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF.

### Slide 56

**Text**

Table of contents / Contact & References

**Layout**

Single-level section divider. The section title "Contact & References" sits left-aligned at about 39% to 48% of the slide height, starting at a left margin of about 6%. A small running header "Table of contents" sits at the top right, right-aligned to a margin of about 6%. No footer or slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Full-bleed halftone pattern: a grid of small square dots in two blues that fades from a flat dark blue on the left (about 0% to 25% of width) to a denser checkerboard of lighter squares on the right (about 60% to 100% of width). The pattern is a single 1920 by 1079 pixel image covering the whole slide. Two tiny 2 by 2 pixel placed images have no visible content of their own and sit behind the title (x 5.7%, y 39.7%, w 49.6%, h 10.2%) and behind the header (x 76.5%, y 6.3%, w 18.5%, h 4.7%). No photographs, charts, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is a dark blue field (#00446E) with a lighter blue halftone square-dot pattern (#005E8E) fading in from the left. Title and header are in pale mint green (#B5F7A0). Typeface is a humanist sans-serif, bold for the title and semibold for the header (close to Fira Sans or Source Sans). Title cap height is about 6.4% of slide height, so its font size is about 9% of slide height. Header font size is about 4.2% of slide height. No shapes, lines, corner radii, shadows or gradients beyond the dot pattern.
