### Slide 57

**Text**

Contact / KfW Bankengruppe / Palmengartenstrasse 5–9 / 60325 Frankfurt am Main / Phone +49 69 7431 - Ext. / investor.relations@kfw.de / Bloomberg: KfW <GO> / www.kfw.de/investor-relations / Team Investor Relations & Sustainability / Doris Kramer - 3531 / Vanessa Wiese - 7995 / Jürgen Köstner - 3536 / Andrea Nickolaizig - 68402 / Dominik Eisenkopf - 2515 / Watch our semi-annual / Global Investor Broadcast / on www.dealroadshow.com / Entry code: KFW2026 / Sign up here / for our / newsletter / service: / Follow / KfW Treasury / on / LinkedIn: / Rely on the safe haven - Investor presentation / November 2026 / 57

**Layout**

Two-column layout split by a thin vertical green chevron divider at about 50% of slide width. The title 'Contact' sits top-left at about 3% margin. The left column holds centred address, phone, email, Bloomberg and URL lines, then a centred team heading and a left-aligned team list. The right column holds a centred header, a boxed photo with an entry-code label, and two rows of text and QR codes. The footer has the KfW logo bottom-left, a footer line beside it, and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Photograph: a television studio set with a presenter in a dark suit on the left and a presenter in a light green-grey jacket on the right, both standing behind a curved light wooden and white desk. Two broadcast cameras on tripods sit in the foreground left and right, with a monitor on the right camera. The background shows blue-grey screens with a city skyline and the words 'Global Investor Broadcast', and vertical grey louvres on the left. Cool daylight, grey floor, dominant colours blue-grey, white and light wood. The photo is a rectangular unbordered image, not cropped, with a white rectangular label 'Entry code: KFW2026' in blue text overlaid at its lower-right corner. Two QR codes: one beside 'Sign up here for our newsletter service:' and one beside 'Follow KfW Treasury on LinkedIn:', both black and white square codes. A thin light-green chevron-shaped line divides the slides columns. A KfW logo in dark blue sits bottom-left. A white bullet glyph in white on white background sits at about 20.8%,26.3% and is invisible.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background #FFFFFF. Title 'Contact' bold humanist sans-serif (Frutiger-like) in deep blue #0E5A80, about 5.7% of slide height. Body text regular weight humanist sans-serif in dark grey #3D4248, about 3.0% of slide height, with bold for KfW Bankengruppe, Team Investor Relations & Sustainability, Global Investor Broadcast, newsletter, KfW Treasury. Accent colour deep blue #0E5A80 for the title, the entry code text and LinkedIn:. Divider line light green #A6E3A0, thin, with a chevron kink. Entry code label is a square white box with no shadow. No gradients, no rounded corners, no shadows.

58

**Layout**

Single-column title at top left, with a two-column table below it: narrow left column (labels, about 27% of width) and wider right column (credits, about 64%) starting at about 30% of width, both within left margin about 2.3% and right edge about 93.7%. Rows are stacked top to bottom with no visible rules, separated only by the row background bands. The KfW logo sits bottom left with the footer text beside it, and the slide number sits bottom right.

**Visuals**

No charts, photos or illustrations. The only non-text visual is the KfW wordmark logo in the bottom-left corner, in blue. The table is drawn as a light mint background block with rows of text; there are no lines or icons.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background (#FFFFFF). Title in bold humanist sans-serif, blue #00609C, about 6% of slide height. Table labels in semibold humanist sans, dark grey #3C4043, about 1.7% of slide height; credit text regular weight in the same grey, with 'Picture n:' and 'Full-page image:' set in bold. Accent colour is KfW blue #00609C for the title and logo. Table cell backgrounds are a pale mint #ECFDED with no borders, corners or shadows. Footer text is light grey #5F6368 at about 1.6% of slide height.
